### [1] [Analytic Criteria for Detecting Bernstein-Sato Roots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14083)
*Yifan Chen,Huaiqing Zuo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究理想（ideals）的Bernstein-Sato多项式的根，并给出判定根的一般偏微分方程准则，应用于一般行列式理想的强单值性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernstein-Sato多项式的根的位置蕴含奇点的深刻信息，但一般理想的根的定位非常困难。作者希望建立可操作的判定准则，并将其用于证明特定理想（行列式理想）的强单值性猜想。

Method: 首先给出判定理想Bernstein-Sato多项式根的一般偏微分方程（PDE）准则；在齐次情形下，将该准则重新表述为一个解析条件，从而把代数上定位根的问题与定义数据的解析性质联系起来。

Result: 对于一般行列式理想，给出一系列根的具体结果，并证明了它们满足强单值性猜想。

Conclusion: 所建立的PDE/解析准则为定位Bernstein-Sato多项式根提供了有效工具，并可成功应用于行列式理想，验证其强单值性猜想。

Abstract: We study roots of Bernstein-Sato polynomials of ideals. Our first main result gives a general partial differential equation criterion for detecting roots of the Bernstein-Sato polynomial of an ideal. For homogeneous case, we recast this criterion as an analytic condition, connecting the algebraic problem of locating roots with analytic properties of the defining data. As an application, we give a series of roots for the general determinantal ideal prove the strong monodromy conjecture for them. Keywords. Bernstein-Sato polynomial, determinantal variety, monodromy conjecture.

</details>


### [2] [Gauss-Manin Connection in Disguise: Holomorphic Anomaly Equations and Higher Genus Real Gromov-Witten Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14189)
*Felipe Espreafico*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个代数框架来研究扩展全纯反常方程，并以五次三维体为主要模型求解至亏格5，给出实Gromov-Witten不变量的预测，并讨论了框架的推广及双三次超曲面的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Alim、Movasati、Scheidegger和Yau的思想，为扩展全纯反常方程建立代数框架，以解决方程中的歧义并计算实Gromov-Witten/Gopakumar-Vafa不变量。

Method: 选取五次三维体作为主要模型，构建代数框架来求解扩展全纯反常方程，并消除方程求解中的歧义，达到亏格5；随后讨论框架的推广，并将其应用于双三次超曲面，计算至亏格9。

Result: 获得了实Gromov-Witten不变量的预测，等价地也获得实Gopakumar-Vafa不变量的预测；这些不变量为整数，且与相应的复不变量模2同余；同时在双三次超曲面上得到至亏格9的相同类型预测。

Conclusion: 该代数框架能有效求解扩展全纯反常方程并给出实不变量预测，且具备进一步推广的潜力，可应用于其他模型如双三次超曲面。

Abstract: We construct an algebraic framework for the extended holomorphic anomaly equations following ideas of Alim, Movasati, Scheidegger and Yau. We choose the quintic threefold as our main model and use this framework to solve the equations and fix the ambiguity up to genus 5. We obtain predictions for real Gromov-Witten invariants, or, equivalently, real Gopakumar-Vafa invariants that are integers and agree with their complex counterparts modulo 2. In the end, we discuss how the framework could be extended and compute the same predictions for the bicubic up to genus 9.

</details>


### [3] [A Functorial Classification of Toric Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14198)
*John S. Nolan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入新的 stacky fan 2-范畴 $\mathsf{StkFan}$，证明其与 toric stack 2-范畴 $\mathsf{TStk}$ 等价，并发展 toric categories 理论作为经典 toric monoids 理论的范畴化，同时给出 Gale 对偶的公理化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往将 stacky fan 构造提升为范畴等价的工作要么存在关键错误，要么仅局限于特殊情形（如只考虑 separated Deligne-Mumford stacks），因此需要一个完整且正确的等价理论。

Method: 引入新的 stacky fan 2-范畴 $\mathsf{StkFan}$，并构造其与 toric stack 2-范畴 $\mathsf{TStk}$ 之间的 2-范畴等价；同时发展 "toric categories" 理论以范畴化经典 toric monoids 理论。

Result: 建立了 $\mathsf{StkFan}$ 与 $\mathsf{TStk}$ 之间的 2-范畴等价，并给出 toric categories 理论及 Gale 对偶的简单公理化框架。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了 toric stacks 的 stacky fan 构造，将其推广为完整的 2-范畴等价，并通过 toric categories 为 Gale 对偶提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Toric stacks enrich the study of toric varieties by accounting for quotients of actions of diagonalizable groups. In practice, toric stacks are constructed and understood via "stacky fans" generalizing the fans appearing in toric geometry. Previous attempts to make this construction into an equivalence of categories have either run into crucial errors or have been restricted to special cases (e.g. only considering separated Deligne-Mumford stacks). In this paper, we solve this problem by introducing a new 2-category $\mathsf{StkFan}$ of stacky fans and constructing an equivalence of 2-categories between $\mathsf{StkFan}$ and a 2-category $\mathsf{TStk}$ of toric stacks. Along the way, we develop a theory of "toric categories" which categorifies the classical theory of toric monoids. We also explain how working with toric categories provides a simple axiomatic framework for Gale duality.

</details>


### [4] [Dynamical degrees of automorphisms of non-projective hyperkähler manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14243)
*Eva Bayer-Fluckiger,Stevell Muller,Matthias Schütt*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文确定了所有已知形变类型、具有不可约特征多项式的双亚纯自同构的动力学度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超凯勒流形上的双亚纯自同构动力学度是复动力系统与代数几何交叉中的重要不变量，但此前对一般形变类型缺乏统一刻画。

Method: 针对所有已知形变类型的超凯勒流形，在特征多项式不可约的条件下分析其双亚纯自同构的作用，进而计算动力学度。

Result: 得到了这些自同构动力学度的确定结果（在不可约特征多项式情形下给出统一结论）。

Conclusion: 为超凯勒流形双亚纯自同构的动力学分类提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: We determine the dynamical degrees of bimeromorphic automorphisms of hyperkähler manifolds of all known deformation types with irreducible characteristic polynomial.

</details>


### [5] [Stability of vector bundles and reduction mod $p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14276)
*Adrian Langer*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明 Sym^2E 的斜率稳定性与 E mod p 约化的 Zariski 稠密性（非强几何稳定）蕴含 E 射影酉平坦，推广 Shepherd-Barron 秩2结果至任意秩，并研究秩≤3与秩4情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广 Shepherd-Barron 关于秩2向量丛的射影酉平坦性刻画结果到任意秩，并探讨能否用 E 本身的稳定性替代 Sym^2E 的稳定性。

Method: 利用模 p 约化的 Zariski 稠密性与非强几何稳定条件，结合 Sym^2E 的斜率稳定性进行分析；对秩≤3与秩4分别讨论稳定性替代条件，并推广到轻度奇异法射影簇上的自反层。

Result: 证明了 Sym^2E 斜率稳定加约化 Zariski 稠密（非强几何稳定）蕴含 E 射影酉平坦；秩≤3时可用 E 稳定性替代 Sym^2E 稳定性，但秩4时不可；结果推广至奇异簇上的自反层。

Conclusion: 射影酉平坦性可由 Sym^2E 稳定性与模 p 约化稠密性刻画，并给出稳定性替代的秩界限及向自反层的推广。

Abstract: Let $E$ be a vector bundle on a normal complex projective variety. We prove that slope stability of $\mathop{Sym}^2E$ and Zariski density of the reductions of $E$ modulo $p$ that are not strongly geometrically stable imply that the bundle $E$ is projectively unitary flat. This generalizes Shepherd--Barron's result from rank $2$ to arbitrary rank. We also show that if $E$ has rank $\le 3$ we can replace stability of $\mathop{Sym}^2E$ by stability of $E$ but we cannot do so if $E$ has rank $4$. We also generalize these results to reflexive sheaves on some mildly singular normal complex projective varieties.

</details>


### [6] [The fifteenth cohomology group of moduli spaces of stable curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14377)
*Amy Bradford,Aitor Iribar López,Andreas Kretschmer,Hannah Larson*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定曲线模空间的低次奇上同调群，证明了亏格2时 $H^{15}$ 等于半重言式扩张，并在条件假设下推广到亏格3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半重言式扩张框架使得人们能显式理解稳定曲线模空间的低次奇上同调群。此前已知 $H^{11}$、$H^{13}$ 情形成立，而亏格1的 $H^{15}$ 情形不成立，因此需要研究更高亏格时的表现。

Method: 利用由 $H^{11}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{1,11})$ 生成的半重言式扩张 $S^*_\omega$，结合遗忘映射的拉回与粘合映射的推前，分析亏格2、3情形下的 $H^{15}$ 群，并用带装饰图给出显式基。

Result: 证明了 $H^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{2,n}) = S^{15}_\omega(\overline{\mathcal M}_{2,n})$ 对所有 $n$ 成立；确定了所有 $g \geq 2$ 时 $S^{15}_\omega$ 中的全部关系并给出显式基；在 $H^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{3,14})$ 维数猜想下证明了亏格3情形。

Conclusion: 作为应用，证明了对若干新的小 $(g,n)$ 对，有 $H^*(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}) = RH^*(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n})$。

Abstract: The new framework of semi-tautological extensions has allowed for explicit understanding of low-degree odd cohomology groups of moduli spaces of stable curves. Let $S^*_ω(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}) \subset H^*(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n})$ denote the semi-tautological extension generated by $H^{11}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{1,11})$, that is, the smallest system of subrings of $H^*(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n})$ containing $H^{11}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{1,11})$ and the tautological subrings, which is also closed under pullbacks along forgetful maps and pushforwards along gluing maps. Recently, it has been shown that $H^{11}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}) = S^{11}_ω(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n})$ and $H^{13}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}) = S^{13}_ω(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n})$ for all $g$ and $n$. Meanwhile, $H^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{1,n}) \neq S_ω^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{1,n})$ for all $n \geq 15$. Nevertheless, we prove that $H^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{2,n}) = S^{15}_ω(\overline{\mathcal M}_{2,n})$ for all $n$. Moreover, we determine all relations in $S_ω^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n})$ for all $g \geq 2$ and give explicit bases for this vector space in terms of decorated graphs. Conditional upon the conjectured dimension of $H^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{3,14})$, we also prove that $H^{15}(\overline{\mathcal M}_{3,n}) = S^{15}_ω(\overline{\mathcal M}_{3,n})$ for all $n$. As an application, we prove $H^*(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n}) = RH^*(\overline{\mathcal M}_{g,n})$ for several new small pairs $(g,n)$.

</details>


### [7] [The canonical class and Chern character operators on Hilbert schemes of points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14491)
*Wei-Ping Li,Zhongyi Shi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文用Nakajima算子给出了Hilbert点概型上Chern特征算子的显式公式，确定含典范类$K_X$及其平方的项系数，进而确定上同调环结构，并给出仿射Yangian作用的替代证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确决定Hilbert概型$X^{[n]}$上Chern特征算子（尤其是含$K_X$、$K_X^2$的修正项）的系数，从而完全确定其上同调环结构，并简化仿射Yangian作用的理解。

Method: 在光滑射影复曲面$X$上，利用Nakajima算子表示Hilbert概型$X^{[n]}$的Chern特征算子，推导显式公式并计算相关系数。

Result: 得到Chern特征算子在Nakajima算子下的显式公式，明确含$K_X$及$K_X^2$项的系数；由此确定Hilbert概型的上同调环结构；并给出仿射Yangian $Y_{t_1,t_2}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_1)$在Fock空间$\mathbb{H}_X$上作用的替代证明。

Conclusion: Chern特征算子的显式表达完整刻画了Hilbert概型的上同调环，并为仿射Yangian作用提供了新的、更直接的证明途径。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth projective complex surface. We obtain explicit formulas for the Chern character operators on the Hilbert schemes of points $X^{[n]}$ in terms of Nakajima operators, determining in particular the coefficients of the terms involving the canonical class $K_X$ and its square. Consequently, the cohomology ring structure of the Hilbert scheme is determined. As an application, we give an alternative proof of the action of the affine Yangian $Y_{t_1,t_2}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_1)$ on the cohomological Fock space $\mathbb{H}_X = \bigoplus_{n\geq 0} H^*(X^{[n]},\mathbb{Q})$.

</details>


### [8] [Characterization of $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor numbers of small rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14506)
*C-Y. Jean Chan,Thomas Hagedorn,Joel Louwsma,Michael Wijaya*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数的可实现性：证明秩至多7的局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度都是$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数，刻画了两变量多项式秩至多8的$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数，并给出变量约化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数是经典Milnor数的二次丰富，是局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度。作者希望理解Grothendieck-Witt环中哪些元素能作为$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数出现，并探讨其与局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度、变量个数之间的关系。

Method: 通过局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度与梯度的联系，在特征不等于2、3、5（部分结果需排除7）的域上，利用Grothendieck-Witt环中元素的秩分类进行分析；并对两变量多项式情形进行刻画，同时证明在温和假设下可通过乘以单位来约化变量个数。

Result: 在特征非2、3、5的域上，秩至多7且为局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度的每个Grothendieck-Witt环元素都是$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数；与局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度不同，一般并非所有$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数都来自两变量多项式。在特征非2、3、5、7的任意域上，刻画了秩至多8的、可作为两变量多项式$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数的元素。在$\mathbb{R}$的子域上，存在秩4、6、8的局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度，不能作为两变量多项式的$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数。

Conclusion: $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数在低秩时与局部$\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer度一致，但两变量多项式的$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数有额外限制；此外，在温和假设下可约化变量个数而保持$\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor数至多相差一个单位。

Abstract: The $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor number of a polynomial map is a quadratic enrichment of the classical Milnor number, defined as the local $\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer degree of the gradient. We study which elements of the Grothendieck-Witt ring of nondegenerate symmetric bilinear forms arise as $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor numbers. Over fields of characteristic not $2$, $3$, or $5$, we show that every element of the Grothendieck-Witt ring of rank at most $7$ that is a local $\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer degree is also an $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor number. Unlike for local $\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer degrees, however, in general not all $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor numbers can be obtained from two-variable polynomials. Over an arbitrary field of characteristic not $2$, $3$, $5$, or $7$, we characterize the elements of the Grothendieck-Witt ring of rank at most $8$ that are $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor numbers of two-variable polynomials. Over subfields of $\mathbb{R}$, this implies that there are local $\mathbb{A}^1$-Brouwer degrees of ranks $4$, $6$, and $8$ that are not the $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor number of a two-variable polynomial. We also prove that under a mild hypothesis one can reduce the number of variables of a polynomial while preserving the $\mathbb{A}^1$-Milnor number up to multiplication by a unit.

</details>


### [9] [A degree-linear upper bound for the Pólya exponent of forms with fixed arity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14535)
*Yulong Li,Colin Tan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文利用概率论中多元超几何分布到多项分布的逼近估计，给出了Pólya指数的一个新的上界 $O_n(d) L(P)/\lambda(P)$，并通过例子证明 $O_n(d)$ 的阶是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pólya定理指出严格正于标准单纯形的齐次多项式乘以 $(x_0+\cdots+x_n)^M$ 后系数最终全正，其最小这样的 $M$（Pólya指数）的定量上界此前为 de Klerk-Laurent-Parrilo 给出的 $O(d^2)P^{(0)}_{\max}/\lambda(P)$。作者希望借助概率论中已知的分布逼近估计，获得阶数更优（关于 $d$ 为线性）的上界。

Method: 将 Pólya Positivstellensatz 视为多元超几何分布对多项分布逼近的推论（de Klerk-Laurent-Sun 已隐含此观点），并直接应用概率论中关于该逼近的已知估计，从而推导Pólya指数的上界。

Result: 得到新的上界 $O_n(d) L(P)/\lambda(P)$，其中 $L(P):=\max|a_\omega|/\binom{d}{\omega}$；并给出一个例子说明 $O_n(d)$ 这一关于 $d$ 的线性阶是紧的。

Conclusion: Pólya指数的上界可以从 $O(d^2)$ 改进为关于 $d$ 的线性阶 $O_n(d)$，且该阶不可进一步改进；这一结果通过概率逼近的视角自然得到。

Abstract: Let $P = \sum_ω a_ωx^ω$ be a degree $d$ homogeneous polynomial in $x_0, \dots, x_n$ with real coefficients. Pólya showed that, if $P$ is strictly positive on the standard $n$-simplex, then $(x_0 + \cdots + x_n)^M P$ has strictly positive coefficients for all $M \gg 0$. The least such $M$ is known as the \emph{Pólya exponent} of $P$. de Klerk-Laurent-Parrilo showed that the Pólya exponent is $O(d^2) P^{(0)}_{\max}/λ(P)$, where $P^{(0)}_{\max} := \max_ωa_ω/\binom{d}ω$ and $λ(P)$ is the infimum of $P$ on the standard simplex. Pólya's Positivstellensatz is a consequence of the classical approximation of the multivariate hypergeometric distribution by the multinomial distribution, as was already implicitly known to de Klerk-Laurent-Sun. By applying known estimates of this approximation from probability theory, we obtain a different $O_n(d) L(P)/λ(P)$ upper bound of the Pólya exponent, where $L(P) := \max |a_ω|/\binom{d}ω$. An example shows that $O_n(d)$ is tight.

</details>

### [11] [Affine realization of tilted Richardson varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14991)
*Xuhua He,Qingchao Yu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文为所有分裂连通约化群构造了"倾斜Richardson簇"的几何模型，利用仿射旗簇中的Richardson簇实现倾斜Bruhat区间，并给出计数多项式公式、全非负部分的几何实现及量子Schubert乘积的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Richardson簇为Schubert类的乘积提供几何代表，其分层由Bruhat区间的子区间索引。本文希望将这一几何框架推广到倾斜Bruhat区间，从而为相关代数与量子不变量提供统一的几何模型。

Abstract: We generalize Nakajima's theory of Hilbert schemes of points on surfaces to Hilbert schemes of points on \textit{orbisurfaces}, and obtain initial factorization based on stratification by stabilizers. The punctual fibre stacks are genuinely different from the symmetric stack, and has natural realizations via motivic integration.

Main category: math.AG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 van der Geer-van der Vlugt 曲线这一类 Artin-Schreier 曲线，刻画其 Cartier 算子结构，进而理解 $a$-number、Ekedahl-Oort 型与有限域上极大/极小曲线之间的关系。

Method: 作者在特征 $p$ 的代数闭域上考察曲线 $y^p-y=xR(x)$，其中 $R$ 为次数 $p^h$ 的加性多项式。通过显式计算 Cartier 算子在 $H^0(C_R,\Omega)$ 上各次幂的像（由不依赖 $R$ 的单项式微分张成），求出 $\mathrm{rank}(\mathrm{Cart}^r)$，并分析最后一次迭代以判定幂零指数与 $a_0$ 的关系。

Result: 对 $1\le r\le h-1$，$\mathrm{Cart}^r$ 的像由不依赖 $R$ 的显式单项式微分张成，且 $\mathrm{rank}(\mathrm{Cart}^r)=p^{h-r-1}\binom{p+r}{r+2}$。最后一次迭代可检测线性系数：$a_0=0$ 时 $\mathrm{rank}(\mathrm{Cart}^h)=0$，$a_0\neq0$ 时 $\mathrm{rank}(\mathrm{Cart}^h)=\binom{(p-1)/2+h}{h+1}$，故幂零指数为 $h$ 或 $h+1$。当 $h\ge2$ 时，$a_0=0$ 与 $a_0\neq0$ 的轨迹 $a$-number 相同，均为极点阶 $p^h+1$ 的单点 Artin-Schreier 曲线的最大 $a$-number，但二者 Ekedahl-Oort 型不同。有限域上，若 $\F_{p^{2n}}$ 上的曲线几何同构于 $C_R$，则它可 $\F_{p^{2n}}$-极大或极小仅当 $n\ge h$，且当 $a_0\neq0$ 时仅当 $n\ge h+1$；显式例子表明第二个界在 $(p,h)=(3,1),(3,2),(5,2)$ 时可达。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了 $C_R$ 上 Cartier 算子的迭代秩结构，揭示了 $a_0$ 对幂零指数、Ekedahl-Oort 型及有限域极大/极小性的影响，并给出第二界可达的具体例子。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime and let $C_R$ be the van der Geer-van der Vlugt curve $y^p-y=xR(x)$ over an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p$, where $R(x)=\sum_{i=0}^{h}a_ix^{p^i}$ is an additive polynomial of degree $p^h$. We determine the image and the rank of every power of the Cartier operator $\Cart$ on $H^0(C_R,\Omg)$. For $1\le r\le h-1$ the image of $\Cart^r$ is spanned by an explicit set of monomial differentials that does not depend on $R$, and $\rank(\Cart^r)=p^{h-r-1}\binom{p+r}{r+2}$. The last iterate detects the linear coefficient: $\rank(\Cart^h)=0$ if $a_0=0$ and $\rank(\Cart^h)=\binom{(p-1)/2+h}{h+1}$ if $a_0\neq0$, so the nilpotency index of $\Cart$ is $h$ or $h+1$. Hence, for $h\ge2$, the loci $a_0=0$ and $a_0\ne0$ have the same $a$-number, which is the largest $a$-number of a one-point Artin-Schreier curve with pole order $p^h+1$, but they share no Ekedahl-Oort type. Over finite fields we deduce that a curve over $\F_{p^{2n}}$ that is geometrically isomorphic to $C_R$ can be $\F_{p^{2n}}$-maximal or $\F_{p^{2n}}$-minimal only if $n\ge h$, and only if $n\ge h+1$ when $a_0\neq0$. Explicit examples show that the second bound is attained for $(p,h)=(3,1)$, $(3,2)$ and $(5,2)$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过对区间 [X/2,X] 中未被 P+B 覆盖的连续整数块进行构造与计数估计，给出 B(X) 的下界。具体构造长度为 ⌊c log X log log X/(B(X)+1)⌋ 的连续整数块落在 P+B 之外。

Abstract: We study the size of sets $\mathcal{B}\subseteq\mathbb{Z}_{\ge0}$ for which $\mathbb{P}+\mathcal{B}$ contains every sufficiently large integer. We obtain $B(X)\gg\log X\log\log X$, where $B(X)=\#(\mathcal{B}\cap[0,X])$ and the implied constant is absolute. This gives a quantitative form of Conjecture 2 of Ruzsa and excludes additive complements of size $O(\log X)$, as asked in one part of Erdős Problem 32. More generally, we construct a block of $\lfloor c\log X\log\log X/(B(X)+1)\rfloor$ consecutive integers outside $\mathbb{P}+\mathcal{B}$ in $[X/2,X]$, whenever this number is positive.

</details>

Main category: math.NT

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hubert Delange曾研究既约分数相对均匀分布的积分，但仅能对q=1求值，并指出q>1的情形尚未解决。解决q>1不仅本身有数学意义，还有助于理解n的φ(n)个互素数相对于[0,n]中φ(n)个均匀分布数的渐近分布。

Conclusion: 论文将Delange关于既约分数分布积分的结果从q=1推广到q>1，并具体刻画了q=2情形的计算结构，为进一步求解该积分及理解互素数的渐近分布奠定基础。

Abstract: In his paper, ''Sur la distribution des fractions irréductibles de dénominateur $n$ ou de dénominateur au plus égal à $x$'', Hubert Delange considers the family integrals defined by
  \begin{equation*} \int_0^1\left(\frac{N(n,t)}{φ(n)}-t\right)^{2q}dt, \end{equation*} where $n>1$ and $q>0$ are positive integers, $φ$ is the Euler totient function, and for any real number $t>0$, $N(n,t)$ counts the number reduced fractions whith denominator $n$ that belong to the interval $]0,t]$. He was able to evaluate the integral for $q=1$ but stated that ''Malheureusement, nous n'avons pas su jusqu'à présent faire cette évaluation pour $q>1$''. In addition to its intrinsic interest, this calculation would help understand the asymptotic distribution of the $φ(n)$ totatives of $n$ relative to $φ(n)$ equidistributed numbers in $[0,n]$.
  In this paper, we first provide some general properties of the integral for any $q>1$ and reduce the problem to its calculation for odd square-free integers with at least two distinct prime factors. We then analyse into more detail the case $q=2$ for which we provide a formula that reduces the problem to the evaluation of the fourfold Möbius sum, of integer valued modular sums related to generalized Dedekind sums.

</details>

Abstract: Let $κ_{\mathbb{K}}$ be the residue at $1$ of the Dedekind zeta function of a number field $\mathbb{K}$. Previous explicit bounds of comparable generality are weaker and require a weak form of Artin's holomorphy conjecture: our bounds improve the constants while assuming only GRH for the relevant Dedekind zeta functions. Abstract: We generalize Christol's theorem---which characterizes algebraic power series over a finite field in terms of automatic coefficient sequences---to completions of domains of finite type over a finite field~$\mathbb{F}_{q}$. Let $A$ be such a domain of dimension~$d$ and $\mathfrak{a}$ an ideal of depth~$d$. In the $\mathfrak{a}$-adic completion $\hat{A}$, choose an $\hat{A}$-regular sequence $\boldsymbol{x}=\{x_{1},\dots,x_{d}\}\subset\hat{\mathfrak{a}}$ and a set of representatives $E$ of $\hat{A}/(\boldsymbol{x})$ that is an $\mathbb{F}_{q}$-vector subspace consisting of algebraic elements. Every element $y\in\hat{A}$ then admits a unique expansion $y=\sum_{u\in\mathbb{N}^{d}}a_{u}x^{u}$ with coefficients $a_{u}\in E$. We prove that $y$ is algebraic over $A$ if and only if the coefficient sequence $\{a_{u}\}_{u\in\mathbb{N}^{d}}$ is $q$-automatic. The proof relies on a careful study of henselizations, which are shown to coincide with the algebraic closures inside the completions under certain conditions. As an application, we obtain a complete characterization of the automaticity of $β$-expansions for formal Laurent series over $\mathbb{F}_{q}$, answering questions posed by Hbaib--Mkaouar and Scheicher--Sirvent.

*Mohammadreza Mohajer*

TL;DR: 该文建立了有限树形式化方法，用于研究齐次q叉滤子下均值中心化实值L¹-BMO上的精确不等式，核心应用是绝对值映射的BMO半范数增长界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锐（sharp）不等式中的精确常数往往难以直接刻画，尤其在BMO这类以鞅/滤子结构定义的函数空间中，局部与全局常数、有限深度与无穷深度之间的关系缺乏统一框架。本文旨在通过有限树形式化，将全局锐常数归约为有限深度常数，从而获得可计算、可优化的结构。

Method: 作者在齐次q叉滤子上构建有限树形式化，证明归约定理：局部与全局锐常数等于有限深度常数的上确界。通过有限锚定（finite anchoring）将问题转化为紧优化问题，并在有理分片线性假设下得到有理解。进一步用稳定中心化（stable centering）说明零根平均条件下上确界在所有深度上保持不变。应用方面研究绝对值映射、Jensen缺陷Carleson泛函及鞅差包络。

Result: 对绝对值映射构造了中心化例子，显示取绝对值会增加BMO半范数，并得到严格小于通用因子2的界。Jensen缺陷Carleson泛函与鞅差包络给出了进一步应用，揭示了不同锚定机制，以及有限深度有理性、稳定化与全局可达性之间的区别。

Conclusion: 有限树形式化为BMO锐不等式提供了统一的归约与优化框架，能将全局问题约化为可处理的有限深度问题，并给出有理极值结构。主要应用表明绝对值映射的最优常数严格优于通用上界2，同时区分了有限深度的有理可达性与全局最优可达性之间的差异。

Abstract: We develop a finite-tree formalism for sharp inequalities on real-valued, mean-centered $\operatorname{L}^1$-BMO over homogeneous $q$-ary filtrations. A reduction theorem identifies local and global sharp constants with the supremum of finite-depth constants. Finite anchoring gives compact optimization problems and rational extremizers under rational piecewise-linear hypotheses, while stable centering shows that imposing zero root average preserves the supremum over all depths. The principal application is the absolute-value map: we construct centered examples in which taking absolute values increases the BMO seminorm and obtain bounds strictly below the universal factor $2$. Jensen-defect Carleson functionals and martingale-difference envelopes provide further applications, illustrating distinct anchoring mechanisms and the differences between finite-depth rationality, stabilization, and global attainment.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Richard Guy在《Unsolved Problems in Number Theory》中提出的一个公开问题，即构造满足特定行列式条件（det A = det A^{[3]} = 1，且所有元素≥2）的整数矩阵。

Method: 首先将十阶情形归约为由一个线性方程和一个对角三次方程组成的非齐次系统，利用圆法得到无穷多个正整数解；然后引入一种加边构造，从n阶过渡到n+1阶，同时保持矩阵及其逐元素立方的带符号余子式向量不变；迭代该构造得到所有n≥10的结果。

Result: 对于每个n≥10，存在无穷多个n×n整数矩阵A，每个元素≥2，且det A = det A^{[3]} = 1；这些矩阵可以兼容选取，构成单个无限正整数阵列的主子块。

Conclusion: 该工作完全解决了Guy提出的公开问题，给出了所有n≥10情形的构造，并通过圆法和加边构造相结合的方法实现了从十阶到任意更高阶的推广。

Abstract: We solve an open problem posed by Richard Guy in his book Unsolved Problems in Number Theory. We prove that, for every integer $n\geq10$, there exist infinitely many $n\times n$ integer matrices $A$ such that every entry is $\ge2$ and $\det A=\det A^{[3]}=1$, where $A^{[3]}$ denotes the entrywise cube of $A$. The matrices may be chosen compatibly, so they can form the leading principal blocks of a single infinite positive integer array. The order-ten case is reduced to an inhomogeneous system consisting of one linear and one diagonal cubic equation. The circle method then provides infinitely many positive integral solutions. To pass from order $n$ to order $n+1$, we introduce a bordering construction that preserves the signed cofactor vectors of both the matrix and its entrywise cube. Iterating this construction yields the result in every order $n\geq10$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三元形式与四元数阶之间的对应关系，并希望突破约化判别式需无平方因子这一限制，使结果适用于所有阶。

Method: 根据定有理四元数代数阶的奇偶性对迹零范形式做归一化，显式构造偶Clifford对应，并结合三元形式的互反性。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了相关对应关系，并将同余数问题中的三元形式通过其关联的四元数阶加以解释。

Abstract: In this paper, by normalizing the trace-zero norm forms according to the parity of orders of definite rational quaternion algebras, we give an explicit formulation to the even Clifford correspondence followed by the reciprocity of ternary forms. We obtain an exact identity between ternary representation numbers and the number of elements of an order with prescribed reduced norm and trace, which applies to all orders without squarefreeness constraint on the reduced discriminant. Applications include a criterion for realizing ternary forms as trace-zero norm forms, a formula for traces of Brandt matrices in terms of representation numbers, and explicit theta series relations associated with local reciprocity. We also describe reciprocal orders and order chains, and interpret the ternary forms occurring in the congruent number problem through their associated quaternion orders.

Method: 通过构造并检验具体的零阶Bessel函数（J_0(a√x)、J_0(ax)、Y_0(x)）是否满足Sigma类的L^1条件，并验证它们是否仍能导出zeta函数方程。

Conclusion: Sigma类并非产生zeta函数方程的必要条件，零阶Bessel函数提供了新的、位于Sigma类之外的重要例子。

Abstract: In 2010, M. A. Chaudhry et al. introduced a class of functions Sigma satisfying an L^1-condition. They proved that every function phi in Sigma gives rise to the functional equation for the Riemann zeta function. They also provided an example of a function outside Sigma for which the functional equation still holds. In this paper, we present several examples of Bessel functions of order zero that do not belong to Sigma but nevertheless give rise to the functional equation for the Riemann zeta function. In particular, we show that J_0(a sqrt(x)), J_0(ax), and Y_0(x) exhibit this phenomenon.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究(k,c)表示模型（源于广义加倍法）中函数的增长性，并理解其与Zelevinsky模型的关系，进而为涉及这些模型的积分收敛性提供理论基础。

Method: 通过将ρ的(k,c)模型与Zelevinsky模型相联系，证明模型中的函数具有适度增长(morderate growth)，并由此给出函数限制到对应复合(c^k)的标准Levi子群上的界。

Result: 证明了(k,c)模型中函数具有适度增长性，并获得了这些函数限制到标准Levi子群上的界。

Conclusion: 作为应用，研究了若干涉及(k,c)模型的积分收敛问题，包括广义加倍法中的积分，以及一个扩展Lapid和Mao关于Speh表示的Rankin–Selberg积分的新积分族。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a $p$-adic field, let $k,c\ge1$, and let $ρ$ be a $(k,c)$ representation of $\mathrm{GL}_{kc}(F)$: a complex representation of finite length whose wave front set has $(k^c)$ as its unique maximal orbit, and which admits a $(k,c)$ functional that is unique up to scaling. We prove that the functions in the $(k,c)$ model of $ρ$ are of moderate growth, by relating this model to the Zelevinsky model of $ρ$, and obtain as a consequence a bound for the restriction of these functions to the standard Levi subgroup associated with the composition $(c^k)$ of $kc$. As applications, we study convergence problems of several integrals involving $(k,c)$ models: integrals from the generalized doubling method, and a new family of integrals which extends the Rankin--Selberg integrals for Speh representations of Lapid and Mao.

</details>


### [70] [Exact Rank of the Largest Good Lattice Point Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15042)
*Long-Hao Xu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明好格点矩阵 $U_n$ 的秩恰为 $\varphi(n)/2+1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对每个整数 $n>2$，最大好格点矩阵 $U_n$ 的秩恰为 $\varphi(n)/2+1$。

Conclusion: 秩由配对结构的对称性与奇 Dirichlet 特征的非零性共同决定，建立了矩阵秩与 $L(1,\psi)\neq 0$ 之间的联系。

Abstract: We prove that for every integer $n>2$ the largest good lattice point matrix $U_n=[u_h:h\in H_n]$, whose columns are generated by all integers $h<n$ coprime to $n$, has rank exactly $\varphi(n)/2+1$. The upper bound follows from the pairing identity $u_h+u_{n-h}=(n,\dots,n,2n)^{\top}$. For the lower bound the columns are centered and the resulting orbit under the multiplicative group $(\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z})^{\times}$ is analysed by means of odd Dirichlet characters; the essential arithmetic input is the non-vanishing of $\sum_{a}a\,ψ(a)$ for odd primitive characters $ψ$, which is equivalent to Dirichlet's theorem $L(1,ψ)\neq 0$.

</details>

Motivation: Van Hamme 的 (G.2) 超同余是超同余领域的重要结果，围绕其 q-模拟和参数化推广存在若干公开猜想（Tang 猜想、Wang-Xu 猜想），本文旨在证明这些猜想并加强相关同余的模数。

Method: 通过建立相应的模 [m]Φ_m(q)^2 的同余式，证明 Tang 的猜想；通过将 Wang 和 Xu 的模 Φ_m(q)^5 的 q-超同余在 m≡1 (mod 4) 时推广到模 [m]Φ_m(q)^4，从而证明其猜想。

Result: 确认了 Tang 的猜想（模 [m]Φ_m(q)^2），并证明了 Wang-Xu 的猜想，将其次数从 Φ_m(q)^5 加强到 [m]Φ_m(q)^4（当 m≡1 mod 4 时）。

Conclusion: 本文完成了两个参数化 q-超同余猜想的证明，推广并加强了已有结果，进一步揭示了 Van Hamme (G.2) 超同余的 q-模拟结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove two conjectures concerning parametric $q$- supercongruences related to Van Hamme's $\mathrm{(G.2)}$ supercongruence. First, we confirm Tang\rq s conjecture \cite[Conjecture $4.1$]{Tang G2} by establishing the corresponding congruences modulo $[m]Φ_m(q)^2$. Second, we prove a conjecture of Wang and Xu \cite[Conjecture $2$]{WangXu24}, extending their $q$-supercongruence modulo $Φ_m(q)^5$ to modulo $[m]Φ_m(q)^4$ when $m\equiv1\pmod4$.

</details>

Conclusion: Kitaoka猜想在全部实分圆域上成立，通用经典三元二次型的存在性仅局限于实二次域这一特殊情形。

Abstract: We prove Kitaoka's conjecture for all real cyclotomic fields, i.e. for fields of the form $\mathbb{Q}(ζ_k+ζ_k^{-1})$ where $ζ_k$ is a primitive $k$th root of unity. More precisely, we show that such a field admits a universal classical ternary quadratic form if and only if it is a real quadratic field.

Abstract: Denote by $Π_γ(x)$ the number of primes $p \leqslant x$ such that $[p^{1/γ}]$ is also a prime. In this paper, it is proved that the expected asymptotic formula \begin{equation*} Π_γ(x)=\frac{γx}{(\log x)^2} (1+o(1)) \end{equation*} holds for $γ> 15/13$, which constitutes an improvement upon the previous result of Balog [2] who showed that the above asymptotic formula holds for $γ> 6/5$.

</details>

</details>


### [75] [A quotient of the Bruhat-Tits building classifying maximal matrix orders over curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15147)

Abstract: In a previous work, we defined a quotient graph of the Bruhat-Tits tree associated to the degree two special linear group over a local field whose vertex set is in correspondence with the maximal orders in a certain number of spinor genera. This graph has been applied to the study of the structure of some arithmetic groups, their presentations and their (co)homology groups. The genera of Eichler orders, which, for instance, are related to the problem of finding a basis that simultaneously splits two vector bundles with the same generic fiber, can often be described in terms of simpler genera via quotients. Furthermore, some embedding problems of orders can be studied via quotient graphs. These results provide a geometric framework for the study of maximal orders over function fields.
  In this article, we extend that construction to a higher dimensional setting through the use of singular chain complexes. Then we describe the set of vertices in this quotient that correspond to split maximal orders and the topology of the corresponding region of the quotient complex. More precisely, we show that these ``split vertices'' fall into a finite number of quotients of apartments by suitable subgroups of the corresponding Weyl group, the symmetric group. We relate these ``quotient apartments'' with the cuspidal sectors defined by previous authors who studied the quotient by the group of integral points of the special linear group. Both quotients are indeed related by a ramified cover that is unramified on cusps, as proved here.

</details>

*Dianbin Bao*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li--Long--Tu提出了两个涉及截断very-well-poised ${}_7F_6(1)$级数及模形式Fourier系数的超同余猜想，尚缺乏统一证明。

Method: 通过形变方法将原截断超几何级数化为合适的终止级数，结合经典超几何变换与Mishev的$W(D_5)$对称性，将两个问题归约为低阶超几何和的同余式，再用Long--Tu--Yui--Zudilin的$p$-adic方法处理。

Result: 成功证明两个猜想；两个情形虽需不同的局部算术计算，但整体证明结构相同。

Conclusion: 该论证为这两个超同余提供了统一解释，并表明形变-对称方法可能具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We prove two supercongruence conjectures of Li--Long--Tu concerning truncated very-well-poised ${}_7F_6(1)$ series and Fourier coefficients of modular forms. The proofs are based on a common deformation method. By deforming the original truncated hypergeometric series to suitable terminating series, we are able to apply classical hypergeometric transformations together with Mishev's $W(D_5)$ symmetry. This reduces both problems to congruences for lower-rank hypergeometric sums that can be treated using the $p$-adic methods of Long--Tu--Yui--Zudilin. Although the two cases require different local arithmetic calculations, their global proof structure is the same. The resulting argument gives a unified explanation of these two supercongruences and suggests that the same deformation-and-symmetry method may apply more broadly.

</details>


### [77] [Post-critically finite polynomials over cyclotomic closures of number fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15257)
*Patrick Ingram,Jorge Mello*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了在任意数域K与所有单位根扩张的域上，给定次数d≥2的单项、中心化、后临界有限多项式只有有限多个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究后临界有限（PCF）多项式的算术性质与分布，特别是当底域扩大为包含所有单位根的扩张时，探究这类多项式的有限性问题。

Method: 通过数域上的算术与动力系统方法，结合单位根扩张与高度理论进行证明。

Result: 证明了对任意数域K和任意d≥2，在K的单位根扩张上，次数为d的单项、中心化、后临界有限多项式至多只有有限多个。

Conclusion: 该结果表明后临界有限多项式在单位根扩张域中具有严格有限性，为PCF多项式的分类与算术研究提供了重要限制。

Abstract: We prove that, for any number field K and and d at least 2, there are at most finitely many monic, centred, post-critically finite polynomials of degree d with coefficients in the extension of K by all roots of unity.

</details>


### [78] [Bernoulli numbers and the integer group determinants of $C_p \times C_p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15262)
*Chatchawan Panraksa*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究循环群 $C_p\times C_p$ 的整数群行列式集合 $S(C_p\times C_p)$，确定了最小指数 $e(p)$ 使 $p^{e(p)}\mathbf Z\subseteq S(C_p\times C_p)$，并给出无条件包含、Vandiver 猜想下的等号条件、首个例外 $p=103$，以及正则素数的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作对循环群 $C_p\times C_p$ 的整数群行列式集合缺乏对最小指数 $e(p)$ 的精确确定与正则素数情形下的完整刻画。作者希望揭示 $e(p)$ 的结构、与不规则对和 Vandiver 猜想的关系，并刻画正则素数下的行列式集合与素数余因子出现规律。

Method: 定义 $r_p$ 为满足对每个不规则对 $(p,2i)$（其中 $r\mid i$）都有 $(2r+1)i>r(p-1)$ 的最小 $r\ge1$。通过 Bernoulli 数的多项式障碍给出下界，利用分圆单位进行构造，证明无条件包含 $p^{p+3+2r_p}\mathbf Z\subseteq S$。正则素数情形下，用 $\mathbf F_p$ 上的显式仿射系统和 $\mathbf Z[\zeta_p]$ 中的主性条件刻画整个行列式集合。

Result: 证明了无条件包含 $p^{p+3+2r_p}\mathbf Z\subseteq S(C_p\times C_p)$；当 Vandiver 猜想成立且 $r_p\le(p-5)/2$ 时等号 $e(p)=p+3+2r_p$ 成立，故对所有 $7\le p<2^{31}$ 的素数成立。首个偏离 $e(p)=p+5$ 的例外是 $p=103$，此时 $e(103)=116$。对正则素数完整刻画了行列式集合；对正则 $p\ge11$，素数余因子在赋值 $p+3$ 处不出现，在赋值 $p+4$ 处的自然密度为 $(p+1)/(hp^{(p-3)/2})$，其中 $h$ 为 $\mathbf Q(\zeta_p)$ 的类数；并证明 $p^{p+5}$ 是这些正则素数下非零 $p$-可除行列式的最小绝对值。

Conclusion: 论文确定了 $C_p\times C_p$ 群行列式最小指数的精确公式及条件，揭示了 Bernoulli 数障碍与分圆单位构造的对偶作用，并对正则素数给出行列式集合的完整描述和素数余因子的密度与最小绝对值的结论。

Abstract: For a prime $p\ge7$, let $S(C_p\times C_p)$ be the set of integer group determinants and $e(p)$ the least exponent for which $p^{e(p)}\mathbf Z\subseteq S(C_p\times C_p)$. Let $r_p$ be the least integer $r\ge1$ satisfying $(2r+1)i>r(p-1)$ for every irregular pair $(p,2i)$ with $r\mid i$. We prove the unconditional inclusion $p^{p+3+2r_p}\mathbf Z\subseteq S(C_p\times C_p)$. Equality $e(p)=p+3+2r_p$ holds when Vandiver's conjecture holds for $p$ and $r_p\le(p-5)/2$, hence for every prime $7\le p<2^{31}$. The first exception to $e(p)=p+5$ is $p=103$, where $e(103)=116$. A polynomial obstruction from Bernoulli numbers supplies the lower bound, and circular units give the construction. For regular primes, we characterize the entire determinant set through explicit affine systems over $\mathbf F_p$ and principality conditions in $\mathbf Z[ζ_p]$. For regular $p\ge11$, prime cofactors cannot occur at valuation $p+3$; at valuation $p+4$ they have natural density $(p+1)/(hp^{(p-3)/2})$, where $h$ is the class number of $\mathbf Q(ζ_p)$. We also show that $p^{p+5}$ is the least absolute value of a nonzero $p$-divisible determinant for these regular primes.

</details>


### [79] [The Norm Spectrum of Barnes-Wall Lattices Below Twice the Minimum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15288)
*Yuhang Cao,Sihuang Hu,Hexiang Huang,Mengrui Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 确定了Barnes-Wall格在最小平方范数\(d_m\)与\(2d_m\)之间的完整范数谱，并用Gaussian整数模型与Clifford酉变换完成了归纳证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Barnes-Wall格\(\Lambda_m\)在最小平方范数\(d_m\)与次小层级\(2d_m\)之间是否存在非平凡的范数取值，即确定该区间内的完整范数谱。此前这一区间的范数分布并未被完整刻画，作者希望给出精确的算术描述并回答该区间范数谱是否非空。

Method: 采用Gaussian整数模型：利用源自Clifford酉变换的格自同构，将一个向量变换为低维格中的一对坐标子向量。通过分析这两个子向量平方范数之差的整除性，并结合矩阵Parseval恒等式，获得对\(m\)作归纳所需的算术约束与平均约束。对每个列出的范数，用两个最小向量之和显式实现。

Result: 证明了：对所有整数\(m\ge1\)，\(\{\|x\|^2:x\in\Lambda_m,\ d_m&lt;\|x\|^2&lt;2d_m\}=\{2d_m-2^j:j\in\mathbb Z,\ \lceil m/2\rceil\le j\le m-2\}\)。由此可得该区间范数谱在\(m\ge4\)时非空。每个列出的范数均可由两个最小向量之和显式达到。

Conclusion: Barnes-Wall格在\((d_m,2d_m)\)内的范数谱被完全确定，呈现为\(2d_m-2^j\)的离散形式，且区间从\(m\ge4\)起非空。证明方法将Clifford结构、Gaussian整数表示与归纳法结合，为相关格的范数谱分析提供了可推广的框架。

Abstract: Let $Λ_m$ be the Barnes-Wall lattice of dimension $2^m$, normalized to have minimum squared norm $d_m:=2^{m-1}$. We determine its complete norm spectrum strictly between $d_m$ and $2d_m$, proving that, for every integer $m\ge1$, $\{\|x\|^2:x\inΛ_m,\ d_m<\|x\|^2<2d_m\}=\{2d_m-2^j:j\in\mathbb Z,\ \lceil m/2\rceil\le j\le m-2\}$. In particular, the norm spectrum in this interval is nonempty for $m\ge4$. The proof uses a Gaussian-integer model in which lattice automorphisms arising from Clifford unitaries transform a vector into a pair of coordinate subvectors in a lower-dimensional lattice. Divisibility of the differences of their squared norms and a matrix Parseval identity provide the arithmetic and averaging constraints needed for induction on $m$. Every listed norm is realized explicitly by a sum of two minimal vectors of the lattice.

</details>


### [80] [Refining the 7/8 zero-free half-plane for Hecke and Dirichlet L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15291)
*Hejian Sang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在假设已有解析输入的前提下，将有限阶Hecke L-函数及Dirichlet L-函数的无零点半平面边界从7/8改进到0.8748867。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进OpenAI此前关于$\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-3})$上有限阶Hecke L-函数的一致$7/8$无零点半平面结果及其向Dirichlet L-函数的转移。

Method: 结合松弛的直接估计与反射分子的放大一阶矩（来自已有的四阶矩估计），并采用两阶段参数论证。

Result: 在相同函数族上，无零点半平面边界由$7/8=0.875$改进为$8748867/10000000=0.8748867$。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段参数论证得到了所述边界，且无需加强逆矩估计。

Abstract: Recently, OpenAI announced a uniform $7/8$ zero-free half-plane for finite-order Hecke $L$-functions over $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{-3})$, together with its transfer to Dirichlet $L$-functions. Assuming the analytic inputs of that work, we improve the boundary from $7/8=0.875$ to $8748867/10000000=0.8748867$ for the same families. The improvement combines a relaxed direct estimate with an amplified first moment of the reflected numerator, obtained from the existing fourth-moment estimate. A two-stage parameter argument yields the stated boundary without strengthening the inverse-moment estimate.

</details>


### [81] [Counting Rational Points on a Certain Bihomogeneous Threefold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15315)
*Xiaodong Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究三重 $V:x_0y_0^2+x_1y_1^2+x_2y_2^2=0$ 在坐标超平面截面补集 $U$ 上的反典范高度计数问题，得到 $N_U(B)=\frac{12\pi-14}{\zeta(2)\zeta(3)}B\log B+o(B\log B)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关注带反典范高度的有理点计数渐近公式，特别是对于 $\mathbb P^2\times\mathbb P^2$ 中由二次型方程定义的三重，希望通过两个投影结合格点计数与一致锥估计，验证 Manin–Peyre 猜想框架下的常数，并明确奇异级数的算术结构。

Method: 方法上利用 $V$ 到两个 $\mathbb P^2$ 的投影，将问题化为格点计数与一致锥估计。主要算术步骤是对纤维奇异级数作平均，其中两个主项由 Hilbert 互反律相联系；用矩估计控制全部坏素数部分，并用形状估计将误差在高度区域内求和。

Result: 证明了渐近公式 $N_U(B)=\frac{12\pi-14}{\zeta(2)\zeta(3)}B\log B+o(B\log B)$，并且所得主项常数与 Peyre 常数一致。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了此类三重上反典范高度计数的精确主项，验证了 Peyre 的常数预测，同时展示了投影、一致锥估计与奇异级数算术方法在该问题中的有效性。

Abstract: Let $V\subset\mathbb P^2\times\mathbb P^2$ be the threefold $x_0y_0^2+x_1y_1^2+x_2y_2^2=0$, and let $U$ be the complement of its coordinate hyperplane sections. For the anticanonical height $H([\mathbf x],[\mathbf y])=|\mathbf x|^2|\mathbf y|$, evaluated on primitive integral representatives, we prove \[
  N_U(B)=\frac{12π-14}{ζ(2)ζ(3)}B\log B+o(B\log B). \] The two projections combine lattice counting with uniform conic estimates. The main arithmetic step is an average of the fiber singular series, whose two main terms are related by Hilbert reciprocity. A moment estimate controls the full bad-prime part, and shape estimates permit the errors to be summed over the height region. We also check the leading constant with Peyre's constant.

</details>


### [82] [Non-vanishing of primitive cubic, quartic and sextic L-functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15316)
*Ziwei Hong*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在非Kummer情形下，建立了函数域上本原三次、四次和六次Dirichlet L-函数的非零比例下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数域上高阶Dirichlet L-函数（三次、四次、六次）的非零比例，改进已有三次下界，并为四次和六次族首次给出正下界。

Method: 采用并改编Gao和Zhao的截断指数方法，结合四、六次情形下的一致扭转一阶矩估计。

Result: 改进了David、Florea和Lalín的三次下界；对每个分支，当导子次数趋于无穷且q固定时，非零比例下极限超过e^{-e^{66}}；得到六次一阶矩的渐近公式，所有结果均为无条件。

Conclusion: 在非Kummer情形下成功建立了三次、四次、六次Dirichlet L-函数非零比例的正下界，结果无条件成立。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the non-vanishing proportions of primitive cubic, quartic and sextic Dirichlet $L$-functions over $\F_q[T]$ in the non-Kummer setting. Adapting the truncated-exponential method of Gao and Zhao, we improve the cubic lower bound of David, Florea and Lalín and obtain positive lower bounds for quartic and sextic families in which every ramified local character has full order. For each family, the lower limit of the non-vanishing proportion exceeds $e^{-e^{66}}$ as the conductor degree tends to infinity with $q$ fixed. We also establish uniform twisted first moments in the quartic and sextic cases, yielding an asymptotic formula for the sextic first moment. All results are unconditional.

</details>


### [83] [Monotonicity of Coefficients of Interpolated Multiple Zeta Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15376)
*Shuji Yamamoto*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了可容许指标插值多重zeta值系数满足单调递减且为正，并通过积分表示确定其零点与符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究插值多重zeta值 ζ^t(k) 的系数结构与解析性质，特别是系数序列 S_d(k) 之间的序关系，以及由此导出的特殊值符号和零点分布问题。

Method: 将插值多重zeta值的系数表示为一个公共测度空间上的积分，并逐点比较被积函数以证明系数单调性。

Result: 证明了系数满足 S_1(k) ≥ ⋯ ≥ S_r(k) > 0；作为推论，确定了 ζ^{-1}(k) 的符号与消失条件，并定位了 ζ^t(k) 在闭单位圆盘内的零点。

Conclusion: 插值多重zeta值的系数具有正单调性，这一性质可用于刻画其特殊值符号、消失现象及单位圆盘内零点位置。

Abstract: Let $ζ^t(\boldsymbol{k})=\sum_{d=1}^{r}S_d(\boldsymbol{k})t^{r-d}$ be the interpolated multiple zeta value of an admissible index $\boldsymbol{k}$ of depth $r$. We show the monotonicity property of coefficients $S_1(\boldsymbol{k})\ge\cdots\ge S_r(\boldsymbol{k})>0$. In the proof, we represent the coefficients as integrals over a common measure space and compares the integrands pointwise. As consequences, we determine the sign and vanishing of $ζ^{-1}(\boldsymbol{k})$ and locate the zeros of $ζ^t(\boldsymbol{k})$ in the closed unit disk.

</details>


### [84] [On strongly minimal asymptotic bases of order $h$ generated by powers of $2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15416)
*Shi-Qiang Chen,Xin-Yue Yan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了判定 $A=A(W_1)\cup\cdots\cup A(W_h)$ 为 $h$ 阶强极小渐近基的充要条件，完整回答了 Nathanson 1988 年提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nathanson 于 1988 年提出了关于此类特殊和集是否构成强极小渐近基的问题，长期悬而未决。本文旨在给出该问题的完整解答。

Method: 设 $h\geq2$，将 $\mathbb{N}_0$ 划分为 $h$ 个无限集 $W_1,\dots,W_h$。对每个 $W_i$ 定义 $A(W_i)$ 为其中元素的互异 2 的幂之和构成的有限非空和集，考察并集 $A=A(W_1)\cup\cdots\cup A(W_h)$。作者推导出一个刻画 $A$ 成为 $h$ 阶强极小渐近基的充要条件。

Result: 得到了 $A$ 为 $h$ 阶强极小渐近基的充要条件。

Conclusion: 该充要条件完整解决了 Nathanson 1988 年提出的公开问题。

Abstract: Let $h\geq2$ be an integer and let $\mathbb{N}_0=W_1\cup\cdots\cup W_h$ be a partition into infinite sets. For $W\subseteq\mathbb{N}_0$, let $A(W)$ denote the set of all finite nonempty sums of distinct powers $2^w$ with $w\in W$. We give a necessary and sufficient condition for $A=A(W_1)\cup\cdots\cup A(W_h)$ to be a strongly minimal asymptotic basis of order $h$. This provides a complete answer to a problem posed by Nathanson in 1988.

</details>


### [85] [On the OpenAI proof that Landau-Siegel zeros do not exist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15483)
*William D. Banks*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文详细阐述了OpenAI一份手稿中关于Landau-Siegel零点不存在的论证，整理为带有显式常数的直接证明，得到(1-β)log q ≥ 3.0×10^{-3}的显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期OpenAI一份手稿断言Landau-Siegel零点不存在，本文旨在详细复述并清晰呈现其论证思路，并给出带显式常数的直接证明形式。

Method: 证明采用插值行列式论证，在由√2在χ所附二次域上生成的二次数域（双二次域）中进行。若存在接近1的实零点，则迫使大多数素数p满足χ(p)=-1，这些素数处的Frobenius同余使得某个非零行列式被这些素数的高次幂整除，超出了其阿基米德大小所允许的范围。核心思想源自OpenAI手稿，但显式常数与表述为本文新作。

Result: 存在绝对常数q_0和δ_0>0，使得对任意导子q≥q_0的本原实Dirichlet特征χ，L(s,χ)的每个实零点β∈(0,1)满足(1-β)log q≥δ_0，可取q_0=10^{10}、δ_0=3.0×10^{-3}。结合Lu、Zaman和Zhao近期的计算，对每个导子q≥3可得下界(1-β)log q≥3.0×10^{-3}。

Conclusion: 本文确认并明确化了OpenAI手稿的论证，给出Landau-Siegel零点不存在的显式常数结果，核心思想归功于原手稿，显式常数与表述为本文贡献。

Abstract: A recent manuscript of OpenAI asserts that Landau-Siegel zeros do not exist. Here, we give a detailed account of its argument, arranged as a direct proof with explicit constants. There are absolute constants $q_0$ and $δ_0>0$ such that, for every primitive real Dirichlet character $χ$ of conductor $q\ge q_0$, every real zero $β\in(0,1)$ of $L(s,χ)$ satisfies $(1-β)\log q\geδ_0$, and one can take $q_0=10^{10}$ and $δ_0=3.0\times 10^{-3}$. Combined with a recent computation of Lu, Zaman, and Zhao, this result yields the bound $(1-β)\log q\ge 3.0\times 10^{-3}$ for every conductor $q\ge 3$. The proof is an interpolation determinant argument in the biquadratic field generated by $\sqrt{2}$ over the quadratic field attached to $χ$. A real zero close to one forces $χ(p)=-1$ for most primes $p$, and the Frobenius congruence at these primes makes a certain nonzero determinant divisible by higher powers of these primes than its Archimedean size allows. The underlying ideas are those of the OpenAI manuscript. The explicit constants, and the presentation, are new.

</details>


### [86] [A linear independence criterion for $p$-adic Hurwitz zeta values at odd integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15525)
*Makoto Kawashima,Anthony Poëls*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了判定p进Hurwitz zeta函数在奇整数s≥3处取值线性无关的新准则，推广了Beukers关于p进Riemann zeta值ζ_p(3)无理性的定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Calegari曾证明p=2,3时p进Riemann zeta值ζ_p(3)的无理性，Beukers用Padé逼近给出了替代证明。作者希望将这一框架推广到更一般的p进Hurwitz zeta函数情形，建立线性无关性准则。

Method: 在一般框架下构造并研究满足函数方程f(z)=f(1-z)的Laurent级数的新型Padé逼近式，以此作为证明线性无关性的工具。

Result: 建立了p进Hurwitz zeta函数在奇整数s≥3处取值线性无关的新准则，并将Beukers定理推广至该情形。

Conclusion: 该工作补充了作者此前关于p进polygamma值线性无关的研究，拓展了Padé逼近在p进zeta函数理论中的应用。

Abstract: In this article, a new criterion is established to obtain the linear independence of some values of the $p$-adic Hurwitz zeta function at odd integers $s\geq 3$. We generalize a theorem of F.~Beukers, who gave, among others, an alternative proof---using Padé approximation theory---of a result originally due to F.~Calegari on the irrationality of the $p$-adic Riemann zeta value $ζ_p(3)$ for $p = 2, 3$. To prove our main theorem, we work in a general setting, where we construct and study new Padé approximants of Laurent series satisfying the functional equation $f(z) = f(1-z)$. This complements our previous work on the linear independence of $p$-adic polygamma values.

</details>


### [87] [The Goldbach exceptional set: an exponent 7/12 and conditional refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15559)
*János Pintz,Ingo Althöfer*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究偶数表示为两素数之和的例外集计数 E(X)，在广义黎曼假设的变体下给出新的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哥德巴赫问题中，偶数不能表示为两素数之和的例外集大小是核心定量问题。本文旨在借助近期解析数论工具改进 E(X) 的上界，特别是利用已证实的统一 7/8 定理作为输入。

Method: 结合 Pintz 关于 major arcs 的显式公式与 Maynard–Pandey–Radziwiłł 的指数和估计，在 Dirichlet L-函数存在公共无零点半平面 Re s>θ₀（θ₀<1）的假设下进行推导；在更强假设（所有非平凡零点实部 ≤θ，1/2≤θ≤2/3）下，改用经典固定模零点密度估计，且不依赖新指数和定理。

Result: 在公共无零点半平面假设下证明 E(X)≪_ε X^{7/12+ε}，其中统一 7/8 定理提供该输入；在零点实部 ≤θ 的更强假设下证明 E(X)≪_{θ,ε} X^{g(θ)+ε}，g(θ)=θ(5−4θ)/[2(2−θ)]，因此任意固定 θ<2/3 给出严格低于 7/12 的指数。

Conclusion: 论文通过整合显式公式与指数和估计，改进了哥德巴赫例外集的上界，并给出依赖零点分布的指数函数 g(θ)，在 θ<2/3 时优于 7/12；后一结果独立于新指数和定理。

Abstract: Let \(E(X)\) count the even integers between \(4\) and \(X\) that are not sums of two primes. We combine Pintz's explicit formula for the major arcs with the exponential-sum estimate of Maynard, Pandey, and Radziwiłł to prove \(E(X)\ll_\varepsilon X^{7/12+\varepsilon}\) whenever all Dirichlet \(L\)-functions have a common fixed zero-free half-plane \(\Rea s>θ_0\), with \(θ_0<1\). The uniform \(7/8\) theorem reported in the OpenAI mathematics release supplies this input. Under the stronger hypothesis that every nontrivial Dirichlet zero has real part at most \(θ\), where \(1/2\leqθ\leq2/3\), we prove \[
  E(X)\ll_{θ,\varepsilon}X^{g(θ)+\varepsilon},
  \qquad
  g(θ)=\frac{θ(5-4θ)}{2(2-θ)}. \] Thus every fixed \(θ<2/3\) gives an exponent strictly below \(7/12\). The latter argument uses a classical fixed-modulus zero-density estimate and is independent of the new exponential-sum theorem.

</details>


### [88] [Bloch torsion and modular combinations of Nahm sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15587)
*Xuejun Guo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 构造一个正定对称整数矩阵A，使得Nahm方程所有解对应的Bloch群元素都是挠元，但相应的Nahm和仍非模形式，说明Nahm猜想中挠条件不充分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Nahm猜想中"所有解的Bloch群元素为挠元"这一条件是否足以保证Nahm和的模性，寻找反例。

Method: 构造具体的12×12正定对称整数矩阵A，分析Nahm方程1-Q=Q^A的解及其对应的Bloch群元素，并考察Nahm和F_{A,B,C}(τ)的模性。

Result: 对构造的矩阵A，对每个解Q（Q_i≠0,1），对应元素∑[Q_i]在ℚ(Q_1,…,Q_12)的Bloch群中是挠元；但对任意B∈ℚ^12、C∈ℚ，Nahm和F_{A,B,C}(τ)都是非模的。

Conclusion: Nahm猜想中"对所有解成立挠条件"并不足以保证模Nahm和的存在，该条件非充分。

Abstract: We construct a positive-definite symmetric integral $12\times12$ matrix $A$ such that, for every solution $Q$ of Nahm's equations $1-Q=Q^A$ with $Q_i\ne0,1$ for all $i$, the associated element $\sum_i[Q_i]$ is torsion in the Bloch group of $\mathbb{Q}(Q_1,\ldots,Q_{12})$. However, the Nahm sum $F_{A,B,C}(τ)$ is nonmodular for every $B\in\mathbb{Q}^{12}$ and $C\in\mathbb{Q}$. Thus the torsion condition for all solutions in Nahm's conjecture is not sufficient for the existence of a modular Nahm sum.

</details>


### [89] [Coprime pairs with equal sum of divisors, assuming smooth shifted primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15674)
*Shisheng Li,Zhong Zhuang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文显式参数化了Erdős问题#824由Pollack-Pomerance论证引出的推论，并在Lean 4中形式化；随后指出OpenAI预印本中关于p-1的论证经符号修改后可适用于p+1，从而在依赖该预印本正确性的条件下得到h(x)=x^{2-o(1)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős问题#824询问满足gcd(a,b)=1且σ(a)=σ(b)的数对计数h(x)是否超过x^{2-o(1)}。已有Pollack-Pomerance的论证将其归约到关于光滑移位素数的估计，但该归约缺乏显式参数与形式化验证，需要进一步明晰与验证。

Method: 首先写出Pollack-Pomerance论证的显式参数版本，将其在Lean 4中形式化：若对每个固定δ>0，存在y^{1-o(1)}个素数p≤5y使得p+1没有超过y^δ的素因子，则h(x)>x^{2-o(1)}。随后检验近期OpenAI预印本中关于p-1的相应陈述，指出在两个小节改变符号后该论证适用于p+1。

Result: 显式参数化的归约已在Lean 4中完成形式化；OpenAI预印本中针对p-1的论证经符号修改后可应用于p+1情形，从而（在依赖该未经同行评审预印本正确性的前提下）推出h(x)=x^{2-o(1)}。

Conclusion: Erdős问题#824的结论h(x)=x^{2-o(1)}在条件性意义下成立，即依赖于OpenAI预印本的正确性，而该预印本尚未经同行评审，且其新的核心估计未获本文独立验证。

Abstract: Let $h(x)$ be the number of pairs $1\le a<b<x$ with $\gcd(a,b)=1$ and $σ(a)=σ(b)$. Erdős Problem #824 asks whether $h(x)>x^{2-o(1)}$. By an argument of Pollack and Pomerance, this follows if for every fixed $δ>0$ there are $y^{1-o(1)}$ primes $p\le5y$ with $p+1$ free of prime factors exceeding $y^δ$. We write this implication out with explicit parameters and formalize it in Lean 4. We then check that the argument of the recent OpenAI preprint "Weighted dilation graphs, smooth shifted primes and totient fibers", which claims the corresponding statement for $p-1$, applies to $p+1$ after a change of sign in two of its sections. Consequently $h(x)=x^{2-o(1)}$, conditionally on the correctness of that preprint, which has not been refereed and whose main new estimates we have not independently verified.

</details>


### [90] [Counting subspaces with prescribed Welch sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15694)
*Kaimin Cheng,Jie Hu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文完全确定了Welch函数在有限域子空间上的和分布 $N_{n,k}(b)$，唯一非平凡情形 $k=3$ 由二元Kloosterman和给出，并由此证明了Hou-Zhao关于Welch函数三阶和自由性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Welch函数的和自由性（sum-freedom）是密码函数研究中的热点，Hou和Zhao近期提出了关于Welch函数三阶和自由性的猜想。作者希望刻画子空间上Welch函数值之和的分布规律，从而验证该猜想。

Method: 设 $q=2^n$，$n=2m+1$，Welch函数 $W_n(x)=x^{2^m+3}$。对每个 $k$（$1\le k\le n$）和 $b\in\mathbb F_q$，定义 $N_{n,k}(b)$ 为使得 $\sum_{x\in E}W_n(x)=b$ 的 $k$ 维 $\mathbb F_2$-子空间 $E$ 的个数。作者对所有 $k$ 求出 $N_{n,k}(b)$，其中 $k=3$ 为唯一非平凡情形，其结果用二元Kloosterman和表示。

Result: 对所有 $k$ 完全确定了 $N_{n,k}(b)$ 的取值；$k\ne3$ 时可直接给出，$k=3$ 时用二元Kloosterman和表达。作为推论，证明了Hou和Zhao关于Welch函数三阶和自由性的猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Welch函数子空间和分布问题，并将关键情形归结为Kloosterman和，进而验证了Hou-Zhao猜想。

Abstract: Let $q=2^n$ with $n=2m+1$ for an integer $m\ge1$, and let ${\mathbb F}_q$ be the finite field with $q$ elements. Let $W_n(x)=x^{2^m+3}$ be the Welch function on ${\mathbb F}_q$. For $1\le k\le n$ and $b\in{\mathbb F}_q$, denote by $N_{n,k}(b)$ the number of $k$-dimensional ${\mathbb F}_2$-subspaces $E$ of $\mathbb F}_q$ such that \[
  \sum_{x\in E}W_n(x)=b. \] In this paper, we determine $N_{n,k}(b)$ for all $k$. The only nontrivial case is $k=3$, for which the answer is given in terms of a binary Kloosterman sum. As a consequence, we prove a recent conjecture of Hou and Zhao on the third-order sum-freedom of the Welch function.

</details>


### [91] [Poissonian Pair Correlation for Bernoulli Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15700)
*Bo Tan,Zhen Zhang,Qing-Long Zhou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究环面上取两个固定值的独立同分布随机游走，给出使配对相关几乎必然为泊松分布的确定性丢番图条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该框架下此前缺乏增量测度为纯离散时的显式例子，且经典傅里叶收缩方法在此失效。

Method: 提出关于(α,β)的确定性丢番图条件，利用返回到1的抛物几何与重叠块的精确协方差公式进行证明，绕开傅里叶收缩方法。

Result: 给出了该框架中首个增量测度为纯离散、满足几乎必然泊松配对相关的显式例子。

Conclusion: 确定性丢番图条件可保证两值离散增量随机游走的泊松配对相关，拓展了纯离散增量测度的研究范围。

Abstract: Let \(α, β\in \mathbb{T} = \mathbb{R}/\mathbb{Z}\), and let \((ξ_n)_{n \ge 1}\) be independent random variables taking the values \(α\) and \(β\) with equal probability. We study the random walk \[X_n = ξ_1 + \cdots + ξ_n \pmod 1\] and present a deterministic Diophantine condition on \((α, β)\) that implies almost sure Poisson pair correlation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first result providing explicit examples in this framework where the increment measure is purely discrete. The classical Fourier contraction method fails in this setting; a different approach is required. Our proof uses the parabolic geometry of returns to \(1\) and an exact covariance formula for overlapping blocks.

</details>


### [92] [Solutions of $x_1^2+x_2^2-x_3^2=D$ with small $x_3$: a spectral approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15706)
*Stephan Baier*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将 x_1^2+x_2^2-x_3^2=D 的整点解按 Phi(x_3/M) 计数，利用自守形式谱理论把该计数化为判别式 D 的测地线到点 i 的距离之函数的和，对平方与非平方判别式分别给出带误差项的渐近公式并改进了已有指数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此计数对应判别式 D 的二元二次型，是相关二次型问题的自然定量形式；已有基本方法仅对 M ≥ n^{5/6+eps} 有效，大筛法与 Kim-Sarnak 界等工具尚可改善误差与适用范围，同时需修正作者早先工作中的主项。

Method: 把整点解视为沿判别式 D 的测地线的计数，用自守形式谱理论计算其到点 i 的距离函数；对平方判别式 D=n^2，测地线由尖点到尖点，用 Hecke 理论归约为单条测地线；对非平方 D=f^2 D_0，测地线闭合，用 Hecke 关系把迹归约到基本判别式 D_0；结合 Kim-Sarnak 界、Deshouillers-Iwaniec 大筛、Duke-Imamoglu-Toth 与 Baruch-Mao 公式以及 Young 的三次矩。

Result: 对平方 D=n^2，任意 n 与两种 x_3 奇偶性均得误差 O(n^{1+theta+eps} M^{-1/2})，theta=7/64，在 M ≥ n^{71/96+eps} 时非平凡，改进基本方法的 n^{5/6+eps} 范围；对非平方 D=f^2 D_0 且 D_0 为奇数，误差 O(D^eps f^{1+theta} D_0^{2/3} M^{-1/2})；对 D=5 mod 8 该计数涵盖全部解，范围由 M ≥ D^{1/2-1/1332} 改进为 M ≥ D^{4/9+eps}。

Conclusion: 论文用自守形式谱理论与数论工具给出了归一化整点计数的渐近估计，显著扩大了非平凡区间并确认了此前工作的修正主项；该工作与 AI 模型 Claude 协作完成。

Abstract: We count the integral solutions of x_1^2+x_2^2-x_3^2=D weighted by Phi(x_3/M), for M smaller than sqrt(D). Such solutions correspond to binary quadratic forms of discriminant D, and the count becomes a sum, over the geodesics of discriminant D, of a function of their distance to the point i, which we evaluate by the spectral theory of automorphic forms. For square D=n^2 the geodesics run from cusp to cusp, and Hecke theory reduces them to a single geodesic. For every n and both parities of x_3 we obtain an asymptotic formula with error O(n^{1+theta+eps} M^{-1/2}), theta=7/64, using the Kim-Sarnak bound and the large sieve of Deshouillers and Iwaniec; it is non-trivial for M >= n^{71/96+eps}. This improves the range n^{5/6+eps} of the elementary method and confirms the corrected main term of the author's earlier work. For non-square D=f^2 D_0 the geodesics are closed, and a Hecke relation for their traces reduces everything to the fundamental discriminant D_0. For odd D_0 we obtain an error O(D^eps f^{1+theta} D_0^{2/3} M^{-1/2}), using the formulas of Duke-Imamoglu-Toth and Baruch-Mao together with Young's cubic moment. For D = 5 mod 8 this counts all solutions, and the range M >= D^{1/2-1/1332} of Friedlander and Iwaniec improves to M >= D^{4/9+eps}. This paper was written in collaboration with Claude (Opus 5.5, High), an AI model developed by Anthropic.

</details>


### [93] [On the inverse Goldbach problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15758)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明素数集加法分解的两个定量结果：二集合情形给出计数阶估计，三集合及以上情形给出对称差的增长下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数集 P 能否被有限个（或近似）加法分解，量化 A+B 与素数集接近时各部分的大小约束，以及多个集合之和无法接近素数集的程度。

Method: 使用 Montgomery 大筛法、Gallagher 更大的筛法、某类乘性函数的均值估计，以及一个次可乘的和集不等式。

Result: 若 A,B 至少两元素且 A+B 与素数集只差有限个元素，则 sqrt(x)/log x << A(x),B(x) << sqrt(x)；若 A,B,C 各至少两元素，则对称差 ((A+B+C)△P)(x) >> sqrt(x)/(log x)^2，并推广到 k>=3 个集合的和集。

Conclusion: 素数集的加法分解受到强烈限制：二元分解中因子规模被夹在 sqrt(x)/log x 与 sqrt(x) 之间，而三元及以上和集与素数集的对称差必有 sqrt(x)/(log x)^2 量级的偏差。

Abstract: We establish two quantitative results concerning additive decompositions of the set $\mathbb{P}$ of primes. First, we prove that, if $A,B\subseteq\mathbb N$ have at least two elements and $A+B$ differs from the primes by only finitely many elements, then $$
  \frac{\sqrt{x}}{\log x}\ll A(x),B(x) \ll \sqrt{x}. $$
  Second, we show that, if $A,B,C\subseteq\mathbb N$ have at least two elements each, then $$ ((A+B+C)\mathbin{\bigtriangleup}\mathbb P)(x) \gg \frac{\sqrt{x}}{(\log x)^2}. $$ An analogous estimate is obtained for sumsets of $k\ge 3$ sets. The proofs use Montgomery's large sieve, Gallagher's larger sieve, a mean-value argument for a certain multiplicative function, and a submultiplicative sumset inequality.

</details>


### [94] [Tribonacci numbers that are palindromic concatenations of two distinct repdigits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15770)
*Khady Faye,Bernadette Faye*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：不存在任何 Tribonacci 数是两个不同 repdigit 的回文拼接形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究特殊十进制表示（回文、repdigit 拼接）与 Tribonacci 数列的交集，受 Fibonacci 及相关数列同类问题的启发。

Method: 结合对数线性形式下界、Baker-Davenport 约化方法以及有限计算验证。

Result: 证明不存在形如 \overline{d_1\cdots d_1 d_2\cdots d_2 d_1\cdots d_1}（d_1≠d_2）的 Tribonacci 数。

Conclusion: 在该类回文 repdigit 拼接数中，Tribonacci 数完全不存在，问题得到否定性解答。

Abstract: Let $(T_n)_{n\ge1}$ be the Tribonacci sequence, defined by $T_1=T_2=1$, $T_3=2$, and $T_n=T_{n-1}+T_{n-2}+T_{n-3}$ for $n\ge4$. In this paper, we study the Tribonacci numbers whose decimal representation is a palindromic concatenation of two distinct repdigits, that is, numbers of the shape $\overline{d_1\cdots d_1\,d_2\cdots d_2\,d_1\cdots d_1}$ with $1\le d_1\le9$, $0\le d_2\le9$, $d_1\ne d_2$. We prove that no Tribonacci number is a palindromic concatenation of two distinct repdigits. The proof combines lower bounds for linear forms in logarithms, the Baker-Davenport reduction method, and a finite computational verification.

</details>


### [95] [$6$ powers of $2$ in the Linnik--Goldbach problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15784)
*Huixi Li,Zhixin Liu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Linnik型Goldbach逼近问题，证明充分大偶数可表示为两个素数加6个2的幂之和（无条件），在GRH下可减至5个，改进了此前结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldbach猜想本身难以攻克，Linnik提出的弱化版本（两素数加有界个2的幂）是可行方向。此前结果中2的幂个数较多，本文旨在减少所需2的幂个数，逼近更强的结论。

Method: 沿用Linnik逼近的圆法框架，结合素数分布估计与2的幂之和的格点/指数和估计，优化对例外集的界，从而降低所需2的幂个数；并考察GRH下素数分布改进对参数的影响。

Result: 无条件证明6个2的幂足够；在广义黎曼假设（GRH）下证明5个2的幂足够。

Conclusion: 所得结果改进了Linnik型Goldbach逼近问题中2的幂个数的上界，在无条件与GRH两种情形下均取得进展。

Abstract: Linnik's approximation to Goldbach's conjecture concerns the representation of sufficiently large even integers as the sum of two primes and a bounded number of powers of two. We prove that $6$ powers of $2$ suffice unconditionally and that $5$ suffice under the GRH, improving previous works.

</details>


### [96] [A note on the $p$-adic inhomogeneous Littlewood conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15809)
*Sam Chow,Owen Jones*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将 Bugeaud–Haynes–Velani 关于 $p$-adic Littlewood 猜想度量版本的结果推广到完全非齐次情形，并在混合设定下部分非齐次地推广了 Harrap–Haynes 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bugeaud、Haynes 和 Velani (2011) 解决了 $p$-adic Littlewood 猜想的度量版本，Harrap 和 Haynes (2013) 也给出了相关结果。本文旨在将这些结果推广到更一般的非齐次情形以及混合设定。

Method: 在混合（mixed）设定下，构造完全非齐次推广，并给出部分非齐次推广的论证框架（基于丢番图逼近与度量数论的技巧）。

Result: 建立了 Bugeaud–Haynes–Velani 结果的完全非齐次推广，并在更一般的混合设定下得到 Harrap–Haynes 结果的部分非齐次推广。

Conclusion: 本文推广了 $p$-adic Littlewood 猜想度量版本的已知结果，拓展至完全非齐次及混合非齐次框架。

Abstract: Bugeaud, Haynes and Velani (2011) resolved a metric version of the $p$-adic Littlewood conjecture. We establish a fully inhomogeneous generalisation of their result, as well as a partially inhomogeneous generalisation of a result of Harrap and Haynes (2013) in the more general mixed setting.

</details>


### [97] [Iwasawa cohomology of pseudorigid families of $(\varphi,Γ)$-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15845)
*Yashi Jain*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了伪刚性族上(φ, Γ)-模的Iwasawa上同调复形，证明了其上同调群的余容许性、完美性及与基变换的兼容性，并应用于Herr复形与Selmer复形的完美性证明及Selmer群秩的解析族变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将Iwasawa上同调复形的理论推广到伪刚性族的情形，从而为Herr复形、Selmer复形的完美性提供新的证明途径，并研究Selmer群秩在解析族中的变化行为。

Method: 研究与伪刚性(φ, Γ)-模族相伴的Iwasawa上同调复形，证明其上同调群满足余容许性、复形具有完美性，并验证其在基变换下的兼容性；随后将结果应用于Herr复形与Selmer复形。

Result: 证明了Iwasawa上同调复形上同调群的余容许性、复形的完美性及其与基变换的兼容性；由此给出了伪刚性族中Herr复形完美性的另一证明，并证明了Selmer复形的完美性，得到了解析族中Selmer群秩变化的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了伪刚性族上Iwasawa上同调复形的基本性质，并成功应用于Herr复形与Selmer复形，进一步刻画了Selmer群秩在解析族中的变化。

Abstract: We study the Iwasawa cohomology complex attached to a pseudorigid family of $(\varphi, Γ)$-modules and prove coadmissibility of its cohomology groups, perfectness and compatibility with base change. We apply this result to give another proof of perfectness of the Herr complex in pseudorigid family. We also use it to prove perfectness of Selmer complexes and obtain a result on variation of ranks of Selmer groups in analytic family.

</details>


### [98] [Nonlinear twists of Rankin-Selberg coefficients in the eigenvalue aspect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15979)
*Yangbo Ye*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究自守L函数系数与固定Hecke-Maass尖形式系数的混合指数和，利用Lau-Liu-Ye的光滑谱二阶矩，证明该和在特定范围内有上界估计，并通过Baire纲论证与Voronoi求和给出具体数值界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究λ_f(n)λ_g(n)e(αn^β)这类混合指数和的估计，是解析数论中关于自守形式系数振荡性质的重要问题，可深化对谱二阶矩应用及指数和技巧的理解。

Method: 核心工具是Lau-Liu-Ye的光滑谱二阶矩（窗口长度可取L=K^{1/3+a}）。第一个论证采用Baire纲方法，通过一个有限导数范数使光滑权重的依赖性一致；第二个论证保持相位显式，运用Voronoi求和与移位卷积估计。

Result: 对任意κ>0，存在β_0>0，使得当1≤X≤K^2、0<β≤β_0时，该和为O(K^{2/3+κ}X^{1/2})。第二个论证在X=K^2、0<β<5/32时给出数值界O(K^{423/224+(5/7)β+ε})。

Conclusion: 论文成功获得光滑混合指数和的渐近上界，Baire纲论证实现了光滑权重的一致依赖，另一论证给出更精细的显式数值界，展示了谱方法与经典求和技巧的结合。

Abstract: Let $g$ be a fixed primitive Hecke--Maass cusp form, and let $f$ vary with spectral parameter $k_f=K$, at a fixed level and with trivial nebentypus. We study smooth sums of $λ_f(n)λ_g(n)e(αn^β)$ for fixed real $α\ne0$. The crucial input is the smooth spectral second moment of Lau, Liu, and Ye, for which the window length can be taken to be $L=K^{1/3+a}$ for arbitrarily small fixed $a>0$. We show that, for every $κ>0$, there is $β_0>0$ such that the sum is $O(K^{2/3+κ}X^{1/2})$ for $1\le X\le K^2$ and $0<β\leβ_0$. We give a detailed Baire category argument which makes the dependence on the smooth weight uniform through one finite derivative norm. A second argument keeps the phase explicit, applies Voronoi summation and shifted convolution estimates, and gives the numerical bound $O(K^{423/224+(5/7)β+\varepsilon})$ at $X=K^2$ for $0<β<5/32$.

</details>


### [99] [Zero density in short strips and short-interval bounds toward Hypothesis S](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15989)
*Yangbo Ye*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文由全局驯顺自守L函数的短区间矩推出短竖直带中的零点密度估计，并将结果应用于带相位的平滑素幂和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Ye–Zhang在短区间零点密度的指数，并把Guth–Maynard的密度界局部化到短纵坐标区间，同时为带相位exp(2πiαn^β)的素幂和应用提供显式幂节省。

Method: 将Guth–Maynard的大值估计与Maynard–Pratt的Type II零点论证适配到Ye–Zhang框架，从自守L函数的短区间矩推导短竖直带的零点密度估计；再通过显式公式应用于平滑素幂和。

Result: 对Riemann zeta函数得到N_ζ(σ;T,T+T^a)≪T^{15a(1-σ)/(3+5σ)+ε}（2/3≤a≤1，7/10≤σ≤4/5），改进7/10<σ<4/5时的Ye–Zhang指数并局部化Guth–Maynard界；在素幂和中相对区间长度K^δ获得显式幂节省，三类应用的最小区间阈值分别为2/3、3/4、5/6；对α替换为mα给出显式频率依赖，1≤m≤K^R一致成立；尖形式应用不需要Ramanujan猜想。

Conclusion: 短区间矩方法既能改进并局部化零点密度估计，也能为带多项式相位的素幂和提供无条件（对GL(2)尖形式）幂节省。

Abstract: We deduce zero-density estimates in short vertical strips from short-interval moments of globally tempered automorphic $L$-functions, by adapting the large-value estimate of Guth and Maynard and the Type II zero argument of Maynard and Pratt to the framework of Ye and Zhang. For the Riemann zeta function, we obtain $N_ζ(σ;T,T+T^a)\ll T^{15a(1-σ)/(3+5σ)+\varepsilon}$ for $2/3\le a\le1$ and $7/10\leσ\le4/5$. This improves Ye--Zhang's exponent for $7/10<σ<4/5$ and localizes Guth--Maynard's density bound to short intervals of ordinates. Through the explicit formula, we apply these estimates to smoothed prime-power sums with phase $\exp(2πiαn^β)$, for fixed $α\ne0$ and $0<β<1$. We obtain explicit power savings over the interval length $K^δ$ for zeta and, using the earlier cusp-form density bounds, for certain real primitive character twists of self-dual $GL(2)$ cusp forms and self-dual $GL(3)$ Maass cusp forms. The smallest interval thresholds in these three applications are $2/3$, $3/4$, and $5/6$, respectively. We give explicit frequency dependence when $α$ is replaced by $mα$, uniformly for $1\le m\le K^R$ with any fixed $R>0$. The cusp-form applications do not require the Ramanujan conjecture.

</details>


### [100] [On bass orders in division algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16002)
*Luis Arenas-Carmona*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将四维局部矩阵代数中关于"幽灵相交"与Bass Gorenstein闭包的刻画结果推广到了局部四元数除代数情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前结果仅在局部四维矩阵代数中证明了Bass Gorenstein闭包与幽灵相交的等价性，但由于局部四元数除代数在偶次域扩张下会变为矩阵代数，该命题在四元数除代数背景下同样有意义，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者将先前关于矩阵代数中"幽灵相交"（即由域扩张上极大序相交后取基域上的点集）的证明思路与工具迁移到局部四元数除代数情形，利用其在偶次扩域下化为矩阵代数的性质进行论证。

Result: 证明了局部四元数除代数中，具有Bass Gorenstein闭包的序恰好是那些可表示为幽灵相交的序，从而得到与先前矩阵代数结果相类似的刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作说明"Bass Gorenstein闭包↔幽灵相交"的刻画不依赖于矩阵代数这一特定环境，在局部四元数除代数中同样成立，扩展了Tu及作者先前结果的应用范围。

Abstract: A result by F.-T. Tu dating from 2011 states that the orders in a local matrix algebra of dimension four that can be written as a finite intersection of maximal orders are those having an Eichler Gorenstein closure. In a more recent paper, we gave a similar characterization for orders with a Bass Gorenstein closure. More precisely, we prove that these orders are the ones that can be written as a ghost intersection, i.e., the set of points defined over the base field of an intersection of maximal orders that is defined over a field extension. Although we proved this result only for the local four-dimensional matrix algebra, the statement makes sense also for a local quaternion division algebra, since the latter turns into a matrix algebra when we extend the base field to an even degree extension. In this work, we prove an analog of our previous result in the latter setting.

</details>


### [101] [Low Lying Zeros of Dedekind Zeta Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16010)
*Timothy S. Trudgian,Sebastian Zuniga-Alterman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进数域Dedekind zeta函数非平凡零点虚部最小绝对值的无条件渐近上界，从Hoffstein的0.87改进到0.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hoffstein 1979年证明h(K)≤0.87+o(1)，Odlyzko 1990年survey中未加证明地声称可得0.54+o(1)，但此后没有伴随公开证明的改进。作者希望给出目前最好的已证明渐近上界。

Method: 选择线性核，先说明Hoffstein原参数已给出改进常数0.825；再利用Stechkin技巧推广其线性核方法。

Result: 证明Hoffstein原参数可得h(K)≤0.825+o(1)，并进一步证明无条件界h(K)≤0.6+o(1)（当n_K→∞）。

Conclusion: 得到迄今最好的已证明渐近上界0.6，改进Hoffstein的0.87并朝Odlyzko声称的0.54方向推进。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a number field of degree $n_K$, and let $h(K)$ denote the smallest absolute value of the imaginary part of a non-trivial zero of its Dedekind zeta function. In 1979, Hoffstein proved the unconditional bound $h(K)\leq 0.87+o(1)$ as $n_K\to\infty$. In a 1990 survey article, Odlyzko stated without proof that a different kernel in Weil's explicit formula could produce $h(K)\leq 0.54+o(1)$. No further improvement on Hoffstein's $0.87$ threshold accompanied by a published proof has since appeared. We choose a linear kernel and first show that Hoffstein's original parameters already yield the improved constant $0.825$. We then generalise his linear kernel method using Stechkin's trick and prove the unconditional bound $h(K)\leq 0.6+o(1)$ as $n_K\to\infty$, giving the best proved asymptotic bound to date.

</details>


### [102] [The Multiplicity One Defect and Congruence-Modular Exponent Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16092)
*Mohammad Masih Hamidi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入"重数一缺陷"这一量，用于度量 Shimura 曲线 Jacobian 在 Hecke 代数极大理想局部化处重数一性质的失效程度，并给出其显式上界及与同余指数、模指数、Gorenstein 性质的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重数一（multiplicity one）是模形式与 Hecke 代数理论中的重要性质，其失效与同余现象密切相关。作者希望定量刻画这种失效，以便更精确地理解局部 Hecke 代数的结构及其对模 abelian 簇算术性质的影响。

Method: 针对不定有理四元数代数（判别式 D、水平 M）对应的 Shimura 曲线 X^D(M) 的 Jacobian，在 Hecke 代数的极大理想处进行局部化；通过在局部 Tate 模中构造特定格，建立重数一缺陷与特征形式间同余、以及各新形式对应的格—Tate 模差异之间的显式上界关系；并考察其与 Gorenstein 性质的关联。

Result: 得到了重数一缺陷的显式上界（由特征形式间的同余及新形式的格—Tate 模差异给出）；证明了新形式对应模 abelian 簇的局部同余指数与模指数之比被重数一缺陷所上界；给出了水平 126 的显式例子：尽管重数一失效，局部 Hecke 代数仍为 Gorenstein，且该例中重数一缺陷为 3，恰等于局部同余—模指数比。

Conclusion: 重数一缺陷可定量刻画重数一的失效，并统一联系同余指数、模指数与 Gorenstein 性质；重数一失效与 Gorenstein 性质并不等价，存在重数一失效但局部 Hecke 代数仍 Gorenstein 的实例。

Abstract: We introduce a multiplicity one defect that quantitatively measures the
  failure of multiplicity one for the Jacobian of the Shimura curve
  \(X^D(M)\) attached to an indefinite rational quaternion algebra of
  discriminant \(D\) and level \(M\), localized at a maximal ideal of the
  Hecke algebra. By constructing a certain lattice inside the local Tate module, we obtain an explicit upper bound for the defect in terms of congruences between eigenforms and the lattice-Tate module discrepancies associated with the individual newforms. We then show that the
  local ratio between the congruence exponent and the modular exponent of
  modular abelian varieties attached to newforms is bounded above by the
  multiplicity one defect.
  Finally, we examine the relation with the Gorenstein property
  and give an explicit example at level \(126\) where the local
  Hecke algebra is Gorenstein even though multiplicity one fails.
  In this example, we compute the multiplicity one defect to be
  \(3\), which is also the local congruence--modular exponent ratio.

</details>


### [103] [Cohen-Lenstra heuristics and vanishing of zeta functions for superelliptic curves over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16112)
*Hyun Jong Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于超椭圆曲线函数域类群结构分布的Cohen-Lenstra型结果，以及zeta函数在固定复数处取零的超椭圆曲线稀疏性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数域上类群结构的分布规律以及zeta函数零点分布，推广已有的Cohen-Lenstra型结果与稀疏性结果。

Method: 通过对特定Hurwitz概型进行点计数，推广Ellenberg-Venkatesh-Westerland与Ellenberg-Li-Shusterman的技术；并借助Venkataramana的算术性定理证明酉大单值定理，推广Yu与Achter-Pries的大单值定理。

Result: 建立了超椭圆曲线函数域类群结构的Cohen-Lenstra型分布结果，并证明了zeta函数在固定复数处取零的超椭圆曲线具有稀疏性。

Conclusion: 点计数技术与酉大单值定理是研究函数域类群分布和zeta函数零点稀疏性的有效工具，相关结果推广了已有工作。

Abstract: We prove a Cohen-Lenstra type result commenting on the distribution of class group structures amongst function fields of superelliptic curves over finite fields. We also prove a result commenting on the sparsity of such superelliptic curves whose zeta functions vanish at a fixed complex number. These results are proven via point-counting techniques on certain Hurwitz schemes generalizing techniques of Ellenberg-Venkatesh-Westerland and Ellenberg-Li-Shusterman. To obtain these point counts, We prove a unitary big monodromy theorem generalizing big monodromy theorems of Yu and Achter-Pries by appealing to an arithmeticity theorem of Venkataramana.

</details>


### [104] [Wasserstein rates of all orders for the Sato-Tate law of Kloosterman sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16169)
*Amir M. Rahimi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了归一化Kloosterman和的分布μ_q与Sato-Tate律μ_ST之间的Wasserstein距离W_s，证明了当1≤s≤2时W_s≪q^{-1/3}，当2≤s<∞时W_s≪q^{-1/6-1/(3s)}，以及W_∞≪q^{-1/6}，回答了Kowalski和Untrau的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kowalski和Untrau提出了关于归一化Kloosterman和分布与Sato-Tate律之间Wasserstein距离的定量估计问题，本文旨在给出这一问题的完整解答，并在更一般的条件下建立Chebyshev矩满足Katz型界的分布与Sato-Tate律之间的距离上界。

Method: 证明使用了分位数耦合（quantile coupling），它来自两个不同区间上的Stieltjes和，其单元格配对以平衡两个测度，并且对所有s同时最优；还使用了相对于μ_ST的显式W_2平滑不等式；以及具有平方根边缘的分布中W_s与Kolmogorov距离之间的精确比较。

Result: 证明了W_s(μ_q,μ_ST)≪q^{-1/3}（1≤s≤2），W_s(μ_q,μ_ST)≪q^{-1/6-1/(3s)}（2≤s<∞），以及W_∞(μ_q,μ_ST)≪q^{-1/6}。当s=2时的界也可由Borda和Cuenin的平滑不等式推出；当s>2及s=∞时本文的界更强。最后表明q^{-1/6}是利用Niederreiter差异界所能得到W_∞的最佳速率。数值上，对于素数10^3<p<10^6，所有距离似乎都以p^{-1/2}衰减。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了Kowalski和Untrau的问题，给出了Kloosterman和分布与Sato-Tate律之间Wasserstein距离的最优上界；所发展的分位数耦合方法对所有s同时最优，且对更一般的Katz型Chebyshev矩分布也适用。数值实验表明实际衰减速率可能更快，约为p^{-1/2}。

Abstract: Let $μ_q$ be the distribution of the normalized Kloosterman sums $\mathrm{Kl}(a;q)$, $a\in\mathbb{F}_q^\times$, and let $μ_{\mathrm{ST}}$ be the Sato--Tate law. We prove $W_s(μ_q,μ_{\mathrm{ST}})\ll q^{-1/3}$ for $1\le s\le2$, $W_s(μ_q,μ_{\mathrm{ST}})\ll q^{-1/6-1/(3s)}$ for $2\le s<\infty$, and $W_\infty(μ_q,μ_{\mathrm{ST}})\ll q^{-1/6}$, answering a question of Kowalski and Untrau. More generally, we bound $W_s(μ,μ_{\mathrm{ST}})$ for all $s$ whenever the Chebyshev moments of $μ$ satisfy bounds of Katz type. For $s=2$ the bound also follows from a smoothing inequality of Borda and Cuenin; for $s>2$ and $s=\infty$ our bounds are stronger. The proof uses the quantile coupling, which arises from Stieltjes sums over two different intervals whose cells are paired so as to balance two measures, and which is optimal for all $s$ simultaneously; an explicit $W_2$ smoothing inequality relative to $μ_{\mathrm{ST}}$; and a sharp comparison between $W_s$ and the Kolmogorov distance for laws with square-root edges. The last result shows that $q^{-1/6}$ is the best rate for $W_\infty$ obtainable from Niederreiter's discrepancy bound. Numerically, for primes $10^3<p<10^6$, all these distances appear to decay like $p^{-1/2}$.

</details>


### [105] [Irreducible components of crystabelline deformation rings: the wildly ramified case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16225)
*Jiawei An*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用ghost series研究Kisin潜在晶体形变环的不可约分支，对野生分歧主序列惯性类型和正则Hodge–Tate权，通过crystabelline斜率区分分支并确定其总数，进而结合Breuil–Mézard猜想计算每个分支特殊纤维的Hilbert–Samuel重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可约且非常通用的模p伽罗瓦表示\barρ的潜在晶体形变环的不可约分支结构，特别是区分分支并确定其数量与重数，这是p进Langlands纲领和形变理论中的重要问题。

Method: 使用ghost series方法，针对野生分歧主序列惯性类型和任意正则Hodge–Tate权，通过分支一般纤维上的crystabelline斜率来区分不可约分支；并结合Breuil–Mézard猜想得到的特殊纤维总Hilbert–Samuel重数的显式公式。

Result: 确定了不可约分支的总数，并计算出每个分支特殊纤维的Hilbert–Samuel重数。当\barρ非分裂时每个重数为1；当\barρ分裂时重数为1或2，取决于对应分支的一般纤维是否参数化可约或不可约伽罗瓦表示。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了Kisin潜在晶体形变环的不可约分支结构及其特殊纤维的重数，结果依赖于\barρ是否分裂以及分支一般纤维所参数化的表示类型。

Abstract: Let $p\geq11$ be a prime, and let
  $\barρ:\Gal(\bar\QQ_p/\QQ_p)\rightarrow\GL_2(\bar\FF_p)$
  be a reducible and very generic representation.
  In this paper, we use ghost series \cite{Lghost2} to study
  the irreducible components of Kisin's potentially crystalline
  deformation rings of $\barρ$.
  For wildly ramified principal series inertial types and
  arbitrary regular Hodge--Tate weights, we distinguish
  the components by the crystabelline slopes on their generic
  fibers and determine the total number of irreducible components.
  Combining this with an explicit formula for the total
  Hilbert--Samuel multiplicity of the special fiber
  obtained from the Breuil--Mézard conjecture,
  we compute the Hilbert--Samuel multiplicity of the special fiber of each
  component. Every such multiplicity is one when $\barρ$ is non-split, and is either one or two when $\barρ$ is split, according to whether the generic fiber of the corresponding component parametrizes
  reducible or irreducible Galois representations.

</details>


### [106] [A Lean formalisation of the disproof of Ramaekers's conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16323)
*Jeremy Tan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Redhill——一个对 Hölzl、Kleine 和 Stephan 关于强 n-猜想改进下界论文的 Lean 形式化，并讨论了设计决策、上游贡献、与 LLM 辅助项目的对比及良好形式化风格准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对强 n-猜想改进下界这一数学结果进行 Lean 形式化，以验证证明正确性、发现非形式化证明中的错误与遗漏，并探索良好 Lean 形式化的设计与风格准则。

Method: 通过精心的类型与数据结构设计决策，使论文中的命题具有规范化（canonical）版本，并由此给出最直接的证明；在形式化过程中识别并修正原非形式化证明中的错误、遗漏与优化机会；部分引理上游至 mathlib；同时与 LLM 辅助的项目（包括将结果扩展到 n=4 的工作）进行对比。

Result: 完成了 Redhill 库，覆盖原论文的主要结果；发现并处理了非形式化证明中的诸多错误、遗漏和优化点；部分引理被上游至 mathlib；Redhill 在无任何 LLM 辅助下完成。

Conclusion: Redhill 展示了无 LLM 辅助的 Lean 形式化项目的可行性，并通过对上游化权衡的讨论及与 LLM 辅助项目的对比，提出了界定良好 Lean 形式化的设计与风格准则。

Abstract: We describe Redhill, a Lean formalisation of the major results in Hölzl, Kleine and Stephan's "Improved Lower Bounds for Strong $n$-Conjectures" (arXiv:2409.13439). Design decisions regarding types and data structures are justified as enabling canonical versions of the paper's claims, and from there the most direct proofs of said claims. The formalisation process also uncovered many errors, omissions and optimisation opportunities in the informal proof, all of which are addressed in the final library.
  Although Redhill is meant to stand alone, a few lemmas were upstreamed to the primary Lean mathematics library mathlib. We discuss the tension between upstreaming and leaving lemmas in separate libraries.
  Unlike many other contemporary formalisation projects, Redhill was completed without any assistance from large language models (LLMs). We compare our work with LLM-assisted projects, in particular an extension of arXiv:2409.13439 to $n=4$, aiming to establish design and style guidelines that define good Lean formalisations.

</details>


### [107] [Dynamical generalizations of the two-point Chowla conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16394)
*Biao Wang,Shaoyun Yi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Charamaras与Richter关于连续素数Omega函数渐近独立性的猜想的两点情形，并由此得到两点Chowla猜想的一个动力系统推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chowla猜想关于Liouville函数在连续自然数上渐近不相关，其两点情形近期由OpenAI解决；2026年Charamaras与Richter提出了将其推广到连续素数Omega函数渐近独立的猜想。本文旨在推进这一猜想的两点情形。

Method: 证明Charamaras-Richter猜想的两点情形，并将其与Chowla型问题及动力系统推广相联系。

Result: 证明了该猜想的两点情形，并作为推论得到两点Chowla猜想的动力系统推广。

Conclusion: Charamaras-Richter猜想的两点情形成立，同时给出两点Chowla猜想的一个动力系统推广。

Abstract: In 1965, Chowla conjectured that the values of the Liouville function become asymptotically uncorrelated at consecutive natural numbers. Recently, the two-point case is resolved by OpenAI. In 2026, Charamaras and Richter conjectured a generalization of the Chowla conjecture to the asymptotic independence of the consecutive prime Omega functions. In this article, we prove the two-point case of their conjecture. As a consequence, we obtain a dynamical generalization of the two-point Chowla conjecture.

</details>


### [108] [Stable reduction and large Nielsen orbits on generalized Markoff surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16407)
*William Y. Chen*

Main category: math.NT
