TL;DR: 该论文证明了 $\text{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ 在广义 Markoff 曲面 $\mathbb{F}_p$-点上的轨道基数下界为 $\ge p$，从而（除有限个素数外）验证了 McCullough-Wanderley 分类猜想，并完整分类了仅在原点上分歧的 Hurwitz 栈的连通分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广 Bourgain-Gamburd-Sarnak 及作者在 Markoff 情形（$\tau=-2$）中的早期工作，解决 McCullough-Wanderley（2013）分类猜想，并刻画 Hurwitz 栈的连通分支。

Method: 研究附属于 $\text{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$-轨道的非合同模曲线，利用其模解释（参数化带有 $\text{PSL}(2,\mathbb{F}_p)$-覆盖的椭圆曲线）证明这些模曲线作为 $j$-线覆盖在 $p$ 上方某处有坏约化，从而得到覆盖次数下界；关键工具包括 Bouw-Wewers 关于三点 $\text{PSL}(2,\mathbb{F}_p)$-覆盖坏约化的结果以及 Henrio、Obus、Raynaud、Wewers 的一般稳定约化理论。

Result: 除 Dubrovin-Mazzocco 发现的某例外有限 $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$-点集的约化外，每个轨道基数 $\ge p$；结合 Kingsbury-Neuschotz 的最新结果，对所有但有限多个素数 $p$ 建立了分类猜想。

Conclusion: 轨道基数下界成立，进而在几乎全部素数上完成了 McCullough-Wanderley 猜想的证明以及 Hurwitz 栈连通分支的完整分类。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds on the orbit cardinalities of $\text{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ on the $\mathbb{F}_p$-points of the generalized Markoff surfaces given by $x^2 + y^2 + z^2 - xyz = τ+2$. We show that outside of the reductions of a certain exceptional finite set of $\overline{\mathbb{Q}}$-points found by Dubrovin and Mazzocco, every orbit has cardinality $\ge p$. Together with a recent result of Nathaniel Kingsbury-Neuschotz, this establishes, for all but finitely many primes $p$, the classification conjectures of McCullough-Wanderley (2013), and gives a complete classification of the connected components of the Hurwitz stack of $\text{SL}(2,\mathbb{F}_p)$-covers of elliptic curves, only branched above the origin. This extends earlier work of Bourgain-Gamburd-Sarnak and the author in the Markoff case $τ= -2$.
  Our method studies certain noncongruence modular curves attached to a $\text{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$-orbit. These modular curves parametrize elliptic curves with a $\text{PSL}(2,\mathbb{F}_p)$-cover. We use this moduli interpretation to show that the noncongruence modular curves involved have bad reduction, as a cover of the $j$-line, at some place above $p$. This implies that the degree of the cover is $\ge p$, which translates into the desired bound on orbits. Key inputs include a result of Bouw-Wewers on bad reduction of three-point $\text{PSL}(2,\mathbb{F}_p)$-covers of the projective line, and the general stable reduction theory of Henrio, Obus, Raynaud, and Wewers.

</details>


### [109] [Hecke modules associated to certain even ternary lattices of half-discriminant $\mathbf{N^2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16505)
*Samadrito Bhattacharyya,Giovanna De Lauri,Dan Fretwell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究半判别式为 N² 的定型偶三元格所对应的正交模形式 Hecke 模，证明其与经典模形式空间之间的显式 Jacquet-Langlands 型对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文旨在建立正交模形式 Hecke 模与经典模形式空间之间的显式对应关系，延续 Birch 以及 Hein-Tornaria-Voight 的相关工作思路。

Method: 对定型偶三元格（半判别式为 N²，N>1 为奇数且无平方因子）的正交模形式 Hecke 模，应用一系列 Watson 型变换，在保持 adelic 正交群不变的同时降低判别式。

Result: 证明了正交模形式 Hecke 模与经典模形式空间之间的显式 Jacquet-Langlands 型对应。

Conclusion: 通过保持 adelic 正交群的 Watson 型变换降低判别式，可显式实现正交模形式与经典模形式之间的对应。

Abstract: We consider Hecke modules of orthogonal modular forms attached to definite even ternary lattices of half-discriminant $N^2$, where $N > 1$ is odd and square-free. We prove explicit Jacquet-Langlands style correspondences with spaces of classical modular forms (in the spirit of Birch and Hein-Tornaria-Voight). This is done by applying sequences of Watson-style transformations that reduce the discriminant while preserving the adelic orthogonal group.

</details>


### [110] [Bounded continued fractions and quadratic orders in $\Q(\sqrt{127})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16530)
*Shirali Kadyrov,Aiken Kazin,Alibek Orynbassar,Nurdaulet Shynarbek*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究了Mercat两种有界部分商连分数构造的最小周期与乘子阶，给出了$\Q(\sqrt{127})$上一族部分商不超过4的显式公式，并由此得到分式和下界与计数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有界部分商连分数的周期长度与乘子阶，以获知固定迹下的下界以及判别式受限的连分数数量下界。

Method: 分析Mercat两种构造；对$\Q(\sqrt{127})$中一族给出显式公式，用单位群指标描述乘子阶；证明Mercat回文构造的本原周期细化，并结合Hensley计数定理。

Result: 对每个$j\geq1$，本原周期长为$92j+2$，乘子阶导子为$y_{4j}/4$（由$(4730624+419775\sqrt{127})^k=x_k+y_k\sqrt{127}$定义），该序的全正单位群在极大序中指标为$4j$；得到固定迹下分式和下界及判别式≤X的有界连分数数量$\gg(\log X)^2$下界；结合Hensley定理得至少$X^{\delta_m/2-\eta}$个二次域含无穷多个部分商≤m的纯周期连分数。

Conclusion: 显式周期与乘子阶公式为有界连分数计数提供了有效下界，并推广了Mercat回文构造的本原周期结果。

Abstract: We study the least periods and multiplier orders in two constructions of Mercat for continued fractions with bounded partial quotients. Our main explicit result concerns a family in $\Q(\sqrt{127})$ with partial quotients at most $4$. For every integer $j\geq1$, we prove that its primitive period has length $92j+2$ and its multiplier order has conductor $y_{4j}/4$, where $(4730624+419775\sqrt{127})^k=x_k+y_k\sqrt{127}$. The totally positive unit group of this order has index $4j$ in that of the maximal order. These exact formulas give divisor-sum lower bounds at fixed traces and a lower bound of order $(\log X)^2$ for the number of bounded continued fractions with discriminant at most $X$. We also prove a primitive-period refinement of Mercat's palindromic construction. Combining this refinement with Hensley's counting theorem gives, for every $m\geq2$ and $η>0$, at least $X^{δ_m/2-η}$ quadratic fields, up to a positive constant, that admit infinitely many purely periodic continued fractions with partial quotients at most $m$. Here $δ_m$ is the Hausdorff dimension of the corresponding continued fraction Cantor set.

</details>


### [111] [The Davenport constant of low-dimensional boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16562)
*Benjamin Girard,Alain Plagne*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究定义在 $\left[\!\left[ -m,m \right]\!\right]^d$ 上、支撑大小为 $d+1$ 的极小零和序列的最大长度：$d=2$ 时给出精确值与所有极值序列结构，$d=3$ 时给出渐近匹配的上下界，并由此改进低维盒子Davenport常数的最佳已知下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小零和序列的最大长度与Davenport常数密切相关，尤其在低维盒子情形下，已知下界仍有改进空间，因此需要针对特定支撑大小研究其最大长度以获得更精确的界。

Method: 研究定义在 $\left[\!\left[ -m,m \right]\!\right]^d$ 上、支撑大小为 $d+1$ 的极小零和序列，通过分析其最大长度：二维情形求精确值与极值序列结构，三维情形推导随 $m\to\infty$ 渐近匹配的上下界。

Result: $d=2$ 时，对所有 $m \ge 2$ 得到最大长度的精确值及所有极值序列的精确结构；$d=3$ 时，得到随 $m$ 趋于无穷渐近相互匹配的下界与上界，并据此改进了低维盒子Davenport常数的最佳已知下界。

Conclusion: 该研究刻画了小支撑极小零和序列的最大长度，二维完全解决，三维给出渐近紧的界，并推动低维盒子Davenport常数下界的改进。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the maximal length of a minimal zero-sum sequence over $\left[\!\left[ -m,m \right]\!\right]^d$ having a support of size $d+1$. When $d=2$, we find the exact value of this maximal length for all $m \ge 2$, as well as the exact structure of all extremal sequences for this problem. When $d=3$, we obtain lower and upper bounds that asymptotically match each other as $m$ tends to infinity. This allows us to improve on the best known lower bounds for the Davenport constant of low-dimensional boxes.

</details>


### [112] [The ring of values of $E$-functions is not a field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16603)
*Stéphane Fischler,Tanguy Rivoal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明由E-函数在代数点取值构成的环E不是域，更精确地说，它既不是诺特环也不是唯一分解环，并显式构造了E中的非单位元，包括e+1、J_0(1)以及某些参数下的₁F₁(a;b;1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E-函数（如指数函数、Bessel函数）在代数点的取值所形成的环E，其代数结构（是否为域、是否满足诺特性或唯一分解性）此前尚不清楚，本文旨在厘清这一基本问题。

Method: 通过研究E-函数在代数点取值所构成的环E的代数性质，并借助显式构造与证明，分析其是否为域、诺特环或唯一分解环，同时给出具体非单位元的构造。

Result: 证明了E不是域，且既不是诺特环也不是唯一分解环；并显式构造出E中的非单位元，例如e+1、J_0(1)以及满足0<...（原文截断）条件的₁F₁(a;b;1)。

Conclusion: E-函数代数点取值环E具有较弱的代数结构，不具备域、诺特环和唯一分解环的性质，这为理解E-函数值的算术性质提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Let $\mathbf{E}$ be the ring of values taken at algebraic points by $E$-functions. The exponential function and the Bessel function $J_0$ are classical examples of $E$-functions. We prove that $\mathbf{E}$ is not a field. More precisely, we prove that it is neither noetherian nor factorial, and construct non-units of $\mathbf{E}$ explicity, including $e+1$, $J_0(1)$, and ${}_1F_1(a;b;1)$ for rational parameters $a$ and $b$ such that $0

</details>


### [113] [Sparse Lifts of q-Linearized Permutation Polynomials with Explicit Inverses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16619)
*Yi Li*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高阶单位环面提升方法，将 F_{q^n} 上的 q-线性化置换多项式提升为 F_{q^{ns}} 上一般非线性的稀疏置换多项式，并保持复合逆的系数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性化置换多项式（如线性化多项式）结构简单但非线性稀疏置换多项式构造困难，尤其是其复合逆通常难以显式给出。作者希望借助单位环面结构，从已知的 q-线性化置换多项式系统地构造非线性稀疏置换多项式，并同时控制其复合逆的稀疏形式。

Method: 通过从 F_{q^n} 上的 q-线性化置换多项式出发，构造到 F_{q^{ns}} 的高阶单位环面提升。该提升保持复合结构：若原线性化多项式及其逆分别具有系数 (a_i) 与 (A_i)，则提升后的多项式及其逆具有相同系数。随后在二维情形得到非线性二项式提升，在三维情形得到置换三项式，并给出显式逆与对合判据。

Result: 在二维情形获得复合逆仍为二项式的非线性二项式提升，包括 F_{q^4} 上一个二项式族的显式逆；在三维特化下得到其复合逆仍为三项式的置换三项式，并给出显式对合判据及示例。

Conclusion: 该单位环面提升框架提供了一条从线性化置换多项式构造非线性稀疏置换多项式的系统途径，同时保持了逆的稀疏性，可用于显式构造二项式、三项式置换多项式及其复合逆。

Abstract: We give a higher unit-torus lift from q-linearized permutation polynomials over Fqn to generally nonlinear sparse permutation polynomials over Fqns. The construction preserves compositional inverses: if a linearized polynomials and its inverse have coefficients (ai) and (Ai), then the lifted polynomial and its inverse have the same coefficients. As a consequence, we obtain nonlinear binomial lifts whose inverses are again binomials in the two-dimensional case, including an explicit inverse for a binomial family over Fq4. The three dimensional specialization yields permutation trinomials whose compositional inverses are again trinomials, together with explicit involution criteria and examples.

</details>


### [114] [Pointwise improvement of the error term in the higher rank hyperbolic lattice counting problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16668)
*Dimitrios Chatzakos,Panagiotis Dimakis,Christos Katsivelos*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了SL(n, Z)高秩双曲格点计数问题中误差项的上界，证明了几乎对所有(z,w)对成立的改进上界，并给出了一个Ω结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blomer和Lutsko近期显著改进了Duke–Rudnick–Sarnak以及Gorodnik–Nevo–Yehoshua的先前结果。本文旨在进一步改进误差项的上界，并为该误差项建立下界（Ω结果）。

Method: 针对SL(n, Z)的高秩双曲格点计数问题，证明误差项的上界；该上界对几乎所有的z,w ∈ SL(n, R)对成立。同时，借鉴Rudnick–Phillips的思想推导误差项的Ω结果。

Result: 证明了SL(n, Z)高秩双曲格点计数问题误差项的一个改进上界，该上界对几乎所有z,w ∈ SL(n, R)成立；同时得到了一个Ω结果。

Conclusion: 本文改进了误差项上界（几乎对所有z,w成立），并给出了相应的Ω下界结果，深化了对SL(n, Z)格点计数误差行为的理解。

Abstract: We prove an upper bound for the error term in the higher rank hyperbolic lattice counting problem for ${\hbox{SL}(n, \mathbb{Z})}$. Blomer and Lutsko achieved recently a significant improvement of previous known results by Duke--Rudnick--Sarnak and Gorodnik--Nevo--Yehoshua. We deduce an improved upper bound for the error which holds for almost all pairs $z,w \in {\hbox{SL}(n, \mathbb{R})}$. We also derive a $Ω$-result for the error term, in the spirit of Rudnick--Phillips.

</details>


### [115] [On Solvable Automorphism Groups of Function Fields over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16751)
*Henning Stichtenoth,Chaoping Xing,Zugan Xing*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究正特征函数域上自同构群中子群的大小界：证明可解子群与 p-子群满足显式线性上界，并构造函数域塔给出渐近线性下界，同时改进了 Ma-Xing 的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Hurwitz 界只给出特征零情形下代数曲线自同构群的线性上界，而正特征情形自同构群可能大得多，因此需要针对正特征函数域的可解子群与 p-子群建立显式线性界，并研究其渐近下界。

Method: 对函数域 F/F_q（p=char(F_q)，亏格 g_F≥2，常域完全）研究自同构群的可解子群与 p-子群，证明存在仅依赖 q 的显式常数 C_q^{(sol)}、C_q^{(p)} 使 |G|≤C_q^{(sol)}(g_F−1)、|P|≤C_q^{(p)}(g_F−1)；并利用函数域塔构造（包括 Hermitian 曲线上的非分歧塔）给出可解自同构群的渐近下界；此外将方法推广到 p-可解子群。

Result: 上界方面：可取 C_q^{(sol)}=12q^2(q−1)（q≥4），C_q^{(p)}=5q（任意 q）。下界方面：存在显式常数 L_q>0 及亏格趋于无穷的函数域序列，使 |G_i|≥L_q(g_{F_i}−1)；q≥5 时取 L_q=2q(q−1)/(q−2)；当 q=r^2（r≥3）时，Hermitian 曲线上的非分歧塔构造给出 L_q=2r^3(r−1)/(r−2)。应用上改进 Ma-Xing 下界为 B_q≥2(q−1)/(q−2)（q≥5），并将方法推广至 p-可解子群得到亏格的显式线性界。

Conclusion: 正特征函数域自同构群的可解子群与 p-子群满足显式线性上界；通过函数域塔构造得到渐近线性下界，改进了已有下界，并将结论推广到 p-可解子群。

Abstract: The classical Hurwitz bound gives a linear upper bound for automorphism groups of algebraic curves in characteristic zero, whereas algebraic curves in positive characteristic can have much larger automorphism groups.
  In this article, we firstly investigate the solvable subgroups and $p$-subgroups of $\operatorname{Aut}(F/\mathbb{F}_q)$ where $p=\operatorname{char}(\mathbb{F}_q)$ and $F/\mathbb{F}_q$ is a function field of genus $g_F\ge2$ with full constant field $\mathbb{F}_q$. We prove that there are explicit constants $C_q^{(\mathrm{sol})}$ and $C_q^{(p)}$, depending only on $q$, such that, for every solvable subgroup $G\le\operatorname{Aut}(F/\mathbb{F}_q)$ and every $p$-subgroup $P\le\operatorname{Aut}(F/\mathbb{F}_q)$, \[
  |G|\le C_q^{(\mathrm{sol})}(g_F-1),
  \qquad
  |P|\le C_q^{(p)}(g_F-1). \] In particular, one may take $C_q^{(\mathrm{sol})}=12q^2(q-1)$ for $q\ge4$ and $C_q^{(p)}=5q$ for every $q$.
  In the second part of this article , we give the asymptotic lower bounds for solvable automorphism groups. For every $q$ there is an explicit constant $L_q>0$ and a sequence of function fields $F_i/\mathbb{F}_q$ with $g_{F_i}\to\infty$, together with solvable subgroups $G_i\le\operatorname{Aut}(F_i/\mathbb{F}_q)$, such that \[
  |G_i|\ge L_q(g_{F_i}-1). \] For $q\ge5$ one may take $L_q=2q(q-1)/(q-2)$. If $q=r^2$ with $r\ge3$, an unramified tower construction over the Hermitian curve gives the stronger choice $L_q=2r^3(r-1)/(r-2)$.
  Moreover, as an application of our tower construction above, we improve the lower bound of Ma and Xing to $B_q\ge 2(q-1)/(q-2)$ for $q\ge 5$. As another application, we extend our method for bounding solvable automorphism groups to $p$-solvable subgroups, obtaining explicit linear bounds in terms of the genus.

</details>


### [116] [Automorphisms and canonical forms of integer lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16753)
*Simon Brandhorst,Tommy Hofmann*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进Plesken–Souvignier算法，利用特征子格、精化向量枚举及外尔群，加速正定整二次格的自同构群、等距与标准形计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正定整二次格的自同构群与等距计算在实际中计算量较大，原有Plesken–Souvignier算法效率有待提升，尤其标准形计算成本较高。

Method: 通过利用特征子格并精化向量枚举来改进Plesken–Souvignier算法；对于含根（roots）的格，进一步使用外尔群（Weyl group）加速标准形的计算。

Result: 具体实现实验表明算法获得显著加速，并且计算标准形的成本可与计算自同构群的成本相近。

Conclusion: 所提改进有效提升了二次格相关计算效率，使标准形计算不再成为明显瓶颈。

Abstract: We improve the Plesken--Souvignier algorithm for computing automorphism groups and isometries of positive definite integer quadratic lattices by exploiting characteristic sublattices and refining vector enumeration. For lattices with roots, we also use the Weyl group to accelerate the computation of canonical forms. Experiments with concrete implementations demonstrate substantial speedups and show that canonical forms can be computed at a cost comparable to that of computing automorphism groups.

</details>


### [117] [The WZ pair and finite congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16844)
*Xiang Chen,Junyan Liu,Hengfei Lu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了 \(3\ln 2\) 超几何级数的亚纯 Wilf–Zeilberger 配对，完成了 Guillera 与 Rogers 未解决的第 WZ 证明，并由此导出单参数恒等式及 \(ζ(3)\) 的逆二项级数，同时研究了该对数级数有限截断的模 \(p\) 与模 \(p^3\) 同余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Guillera 和 Rogers 此前关于 \(3\ln 2\) 超几何级数的 WZ 证明未能完成，本文旨在补全这一证明，并进一步探索由该 WZ 配对导出的相关恒等式与同余性质。

Method: 构造亚纯 Wilf–Zeilberger 配对；结合 WZ 方法、终止超几何恒等式、Pfaff–Saalschütz 恒等式以及调和和的经典同余工具进行证明。

Result: 得到单参数恒等式；经半整数平移与微分后获得 \(ζ(3)\) 的逆二项级数；对素数 \(p\ge 5\) 证明下截断模 \(p\) 同余于 \(q_p(2)-1\)，上截断模 \(p^3\) 同余于 \(-\frac{7}{16}p^2B_{p-3}\)。

Conclusion: 该工作补全了 \(3\ln 2\) 的 WZ 证明，并揭示了该对数级数有限截断在模算术下与 Fermat 商及 Bernoulli 数的深刻联系。

Abstract: We construct a meromorphic Wilf--Zeilberger pair for the hypergeometric series for \(3\ln 2\), completing the WZ proof left open by Guillera and Rogers. The same pair yields a one-parameter identity and, after a half-integer shift and differentiation, an inverse-binomial series for \(ζ(3)\). We also study the finite truncations of the logarithmic series. For every prime \(p\ge 5\), we prove that the lower truncation is congruent to \(q_p(2)-1\) modulo \(p\), while the upper truncation is congruent to \(-\frac{7}{16}p^2B_{p-3}\) modulo \(p^3\). The proofs combine the WZ method with terminating hypergeometric identities, the Pfaff--Saalschütz identity, and classical congruences for harmonic sums.

</details>


### [118] [CM fibers of Shimura parametrizations and thirteen proofs of the class number one theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16953)
*Jingxuan Geng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了13条与秩为2、导子小于2000的椭圆曲线相关联的Shimura参数化的全CM恒等纤维，每条纤维都给出虚二次类数一 theorem 的证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Shimura参数化的CM纤维结构，并借由这些纤维为虚二次类数一定理提供新证明。

Method: 结合Gross–Zagier公式与Ribet–Takahashi次数比较，证明这些纤维完全可以由已知CM点解释。

Result: 确定了13条全CM恒等纤维，均关联于导子小于2000、秩为2的椭圆曲线。

Conclusion: 每条全CM恒等纤维都能导出虚二次类数一定理的证明，说明这些纤维完全由已知CM点解释。

Abstract: We determine thirteen entirely CM identity fibers of Shimura parametrizations attached to elliptic curves of rank two and conductor below $2000$. Each yields a proof of the imaginary quadratic class number one theorem. The argument combines the Gross--Zagier formula with the Ribet--Takahashi degree comparison to show that the fiber is completely explained by known CM points.

</details>


### [119] [Arithmetic jet spaces and the unramified Zilber--Pink conjecture for subvarieties of abelian varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17325)
*Netan Dogra,Arnab Saha*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文运用差分几何（delta geometry）方法，在数域上研究阿贝尔簇子簇的Zilber–Pink轨迹中、在固定素处具有有界分歧的点，并给出其次数的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zilber–Pink轨迹中点的性质是算术几何中的重要问题。已有的研究多关注点的存在性或无限性，而对点在数域上、特别在固定素处有分歧限制时的次数（度数）定量估计仍不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用delta几何（差分几何）的方法，将问题置于数域上的阿贝尔簇子簇框架中，并对所考虑的点施加在某一固定素数处有界分歧的限制条件。

Result: 得到了Zilber–Pink轨迹中满足有界分歧条件的点的次数的界。

Conclusion: 差分几何方法可有效用于定量约束Zilber–Pink轨迹中的点，为相关算术几何问题提供了新的工具与思路。

Abstract: We show how the methods of delta geometry can be used to bound the degree of points in the Zilber--Pink locus of a subvariety of an abelian variety, if the varieties are defined over a number field and we restrict to points with bounded ramification at a fixed prime.

</details>


### [120] [Distribution of central values of Hecke $L$-functions of $n$-th order characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17144)
*Lewis McGoldrick*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在薄家族 n 阶 Hecke 特征中 log|L(1/2,χ)| 的单边中心极限定理（n≥3），并得到了 2k 次矩（0<k<1）猜想阶的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 L 函数中心值的分布与矩是解析数论的核心问题，本文希望在薄家族中建立单边中心极限定理并给出矩的上界。

Method: 结合 Radziwiłł–Soundararajan 的方法与 Diaconu、Ion、Paşol、Popa 关于带幂次节省误差项的扭曲二阶矩的渐近公式。

Result: 对 n≥3 的 n 阶 Hecke 特征薄家族证明了 log|L(1/2,χ)| 的单边中心极限定理；并得到 0<k<1 时 2k 次矩的猜想阶上界。

Conclusion: 该结果将单边中心极限定理推广至薄家族的高阶 Hecke 特征情形，并对相关矩问题给出了接近猜想的界。

Abstract: We prove a one-sided central limit theorem for $\log |L(\tfrac 12,χ)|$ in a thin family of $n$-th order Hecke characters, for $n \geq 3$. The proof combines the method of Radziwiłł and Soundararajan with a recent asymptotic formula of Diaconu, Ion, Paşol, and Popa for the twisted second moment with a power-saving error term. We also obtain upper bounds of the conjectured order of magnitude for the $2k$-th moments for $0< k< 1$.

</details>


### [121] [Counting matrices with given characteristic polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18226)
*Tim Browning,Fabian Gundlach,Will Sawin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 利用傅里叶分析证明了具有给定特征多项式且元素有界的整矩阵数量的新上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Habegger、Ostafe 和 Shparlinski 近期关于整矩阵计数的工作，尤其是 n≥4 的情形。

Method: 使用傅里叶分析。

Result: 给出了 n×n、特征多项式为 f 且元素在 [-T,T] 内的整矩阵数量的新上界，误差常数仅依赖于 n 且对 f 一致；在 n≥4 时优于已有结果。

Conclusion: 该新上界改进了已有的整矩阵计数结果，并保持对 f 的一致性和对 n 的依赖。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a monic polynomial of degree $n$ with integer coefficients. We use Fourier analysis to prove a new upper bound on the number of $n\times n$ matrices with characteristic polynomial $f$ and integer entries in $[-T,T]$, as $T\to \infty$. The implied constant in our bound only depends on $n$ and is otherwise uniform in $f$. Our bound improves on recent work of Habegger, Ostafe and Shparlinski for $n\geq 4$.

</details>


### [122] [A Half-Constant Upper Bound for Chowla's Cosine Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17160)
*Bryan Bu,Fayzan Khan,Hyunjun Yi,Atharv Jha,Vihaan Vajpeyi,Ishaan Chandupatla,Madhav Nagpal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究余弦和的极小值下确界 λ(n)，构造出达到最优主项系数 1/2 的整数集合，并给出相应的下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Sidon 差集构造仅能得到 λ(n) ≤ (1+o(1))√n/2，主项常数并非最优，作者希望改进主项常数并刻画下界约束。

Method: 结合双侧 Sidon 平方恒等式、Singer 差集、Fejér 核优函数、奇偶偏置删除以及精确基数概率选择进行构造；下界方面将 L^1 范数引入 Bedert 边界估计并给出显式常数。

Result: 构造得到 λ(n) ≤ (1/2)√n + O(n^{1/4}√log n)，即 limsup λ(n)/√n ≤ 1/2；下界得到 -min f_A ≥ cn^{1/4}（A⊂[1,Cn]）与 (-min f_A)^3||f_A||_1^2 ≥ cn；数值验证至 |A|=25,440。

Conclusion: 该工作将 λ(n) 的最优主项常数确定为 1/2，并给出 L^4 判据与重数界约束更小常数构造，同时改进 Bedert–Shankar 的 1/5 指数结果。

Abstract: Let lambda(n) denote the infimum of -min_x sum_{a in A} cos(ax) over all sets A of n positive integers. While the classical Sidon-difference construction yields lambda(n) <= (1 + o(1))sqrt(n)/2, we construct sets achieving a leading constant of 1/2: lambda(n) <= (1/2)sqrt(n) + O(n^(1/4)sqrt(log n)), establishing limsup_{n->infinity} lambda(n)/sqrt(n) <= 1/2. This construction combines a two-sided Sidon square identity, a Singer difference set, a Fejer-kernel majorant, a parity-biased deletion, and an exact-cardinality probabilistic selection. We also provide an L^4 criterion and a multiplicity bound constraining any construction with a smaller leading constant. In the opposite direction, we incorporate the L^1 norm into Bedert's boundary estimate with an explicit constant, yielding two lower bounds for f_A(x) = sum_{a in A} cos(ax): (1) -min f_A >= cn^(1/4) for every n-element set A subset of [1, Cn]; and (2) (-min f_A)^3 ||f_A||_1^2 >= cn for every n-element set A subset of N. The second bound recovers the 1/5 exponent of Bedert and Shankar and improves it whenever ||f_A||_1 is small relative to -min f_A. Numerical values of the construction are reported up to |A| = 25,440.

</details>


### [123] [Holonomic sequences arising from meromorphic modular forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17240)
*Kam Cheong Au*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从亚纯模形式系统构造全纯递推（holonomic recurrences）的框架，核心是模形式的“creative telescoping”类比，将有限维商空间转化为显式递推算子，统一解释了Apéry递推、Beukers构造与Zagier七个零星递推，并产生大量新例，同时赋予序列算术性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前Apéry递推、Beukers的模构造以及Zagier的七个零星递推等例子彼此孤立，缺乏统一的理论解释。作者希望建立一个系统框架，从模形式出发统一构造并解释这些递推，同时推广产生新例。

Method: 引入模形式的creative telescoping类比，将亚纯模形式空间的有限维商转化为显式递推算子，从而系统地从亚纯模形式构造全纯递推。

Result: 该框架恢复了Apéry递推、Beukers的模构造和Zagier的七个零星递推，并在不同权重与群上产生了大量新例子；序列的模起源还带来分母界、无理性陈述和p进同余等非平凡算术性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为多个孤立的递推例子提供了统一解释，并展示了模形式构造序列在算术性质上的深刻应用与推广潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a systematic framework for constructing holonomic recurrences from meromorphic modular forms. Its central ingredient is a modular analogue of creative telescoping, which turns finite-dimensional quotients of spaces of meromorphic modular forms into explicit recurrence operators. This provides a common explanation for several previously isolated examples: it recovers Apéry's recurrence, Beukers' modular constructions and Zagier's seven sporadic recurrences. The framework also produces a large collection of new examples across different weights and groups. The modular origin of these sequences also confers them with non-trivial arithmetic properties, such as denominator bounds, irrationality statements, and $p$-adic congruences.

</details>


### [124] [An Enhanced Higher Green's Function on the Modular Curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17258)
*Nina Sakharova*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了Green函数的一个复值提升，研究其CM值的性质，并证明了在特殊情形下归一化CM值的指数具有代数性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gross-Zagier猜想断言Green函数在CM点的值（乘以判别式幂后）为代数数绝对值的对数，Mellit提出了复精细化猜想，预测Green函数复提升的归一化CM值为代数数模周期的对数。本文旨在研究这一复提升及其CM值的性质，验证Mellit猜想的相关内容。

Method: 取Γ=SL₂(Z)及k∈{4,6,8,10,14}，沿用Zagier由Eisenstein级数E₄的Eichler积分构造CM不变量的方法，对Bergman型核的归一化迭代Eichler积分在两个变量上应用Maass上升算子，得到复值扩张𝓖°_{k/2}(z,w)，其实部为G_{k/2}(z,w)的常数倍；随后研究其归一化CM值对二次型类代表选取的依赖性。

Result: 实部是Green函数G_{k/2}(z,w)的常数倍；将第一变量对应的二次型替换为等价二次型时，归一化CM值的指数仅改变一个单位根；在第二CM点w_*=(1+i√d)/2（d>3, d≡3 mod 4）且第一变量对应二次型类等于其逆的特殊情形下，证明了这些指数的代数性。

Conclusion: 本文给出了Green函数的复值提升并刻画了其CM值对二次型类代表选取的依赖关系，在特殊情形下证明了归一化CM值指数的代数性，为Mellit的复精细化猜想提供了支持。

Abstract: The higher Green's functions $G_{k/2}(z,w)$ on the modular curve are real-valued eigenfunctions of the hyperbolic Laplacian in each variable, with logarithmic singularities along the diagonal. Gross and Zagier conjectured that their values at CM points, after multiplication by suitable powers of the discriminants, are logarithms of absolute values of algebraic numbers. The conjecture was proved by Li, Bruinier, and Yang. Mellit proposed a complex refinement of this conjecture, predicting that the normalized CM values of a complex lift of the Green function are logarithms of algebraic numbers modulo periods.
  For $Γ=\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb Z)$ and $k\in{4,6,8,10,14}$, we follow Zagier's construction of CM invariants from Eichler integrals of the Eisenstein series $E_4$. By applying Maass raising operators in both variables to a normalized iterated Eichler integral of a Bergman-type kernel, we obtain a complex-valued extension $\mathcal G_{k/2}^\bullet(z,w)$ whose real part is a constant multiple of $G_{k/2}(z,w)$. We study the normalized CM values of $\mathcal G_{k/2}^\bullet$ and describe their dependence on the choice of representatives of quadratic form classes. In particular, we show that replacing the quadratic form corresponding to the first variable by an equivalent one changes the exponential of the normalized value only by a root of unity. We also prove the algebraicity of these exponentials in the special case where the second CM point is $w_*=(1+i\sqrt d)/2$, with $d>3$ and $d\equiv3\pmod4$, and the class of the quadratic form corresponding to the first variable is equal to its inverse.

</details>


### [125] [Likely and unlikely ramification of algebraic points on curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17262)
*Bianca Viray,Isabel Vogt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文开创性地研究曲线上任意次数闭点的算术性质，证明秩为0的无点曲线在足够大的素数ℓ下仅有有限多个循环ℓ次点；更一般地，亏格至少为2且秩为0的曲线上点的剩余域往往偏离完全分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲线上任意次数闭点（而非仅有理点）的算术性质，特别是在秩为0且无有理点的情形下，探讨循环ℓ次点与剩余域的分歧行为，以深化对曲线有理点分布的理解。

Method: 通过为阿贝尔簇自乘积构造一个介于Manin–Mumford猜想与Zilber–Pink猜想之间的自然中间猜想，并利用该猜想证明关于循环ℓ次点有限性及剩余域偏离完全分歧的结果。

Result: 秩为0的无点曲线对足够大的素数ℓ仅有有限多个循环ℓ次点；亏格至少为2、秩为0的曲线上点的剩余域往往偏离完全分歧；该中间猜想还可推出其他分裂剖面的类似结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了曲线上任意次数闭点算术性质的研究框架，揭示了Manin–Mumford猜想与Zilber–Pink猜想之间的中间猜想在分裂剖面分析中的核心作用，并为更广泛的偏差现象提供了统一解释。

Abstract: This paper initiates the study of arithmetic properties of closed points of arbitrary degree on curves. We show that a pointless curve of rank $0$ can have only finitely many cyclic degree $\ell$ points for all sufficiently large primes $\ell$. More generally, we show that the residue fields of points on curves of genus at least $2$ and rank $0$ are often biased away from total ramification. The proof technique illuminates a natural intermediate conjecture between the Manin--Mumford conjecture and the Zilber--Pink conjecture for self-products of abelian varieties. This intermediate conjecture implies analogous results for other splitting profiles.

</details>


### [126] [Denominators in the Farey Sequence, the Stern Sequence, and the Frobenius Problem with Relatively Prime Multipliers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17267)
*Grace Natalie Jaffe*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将Frobenius问题推广到系数本身也需互素的情形，证明了最大不可表示整数仍然存在，并研究了其取值与性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Frobenius问题已解决，但在系数也受互素限制的新条件下成为开放问题，并与Stern序列、Farey序列及Jacobsthal函数相关。

Method: 在原有非负系数线性组合的基础上，增加系数本身互素的限制，对由此产生的最大不可表示整数进行存在性证明，并分析其关于a、b的函数性质。

Result: 证明了在该新限制下，最大不可表示整数依然始终存在。

Conclusion: 该推广问题具有良好结构，其最大不可表示整数的取值及性质与Stern序列、Farey序列和Jacobsthal函数存在联系。

Abstract: The Frobenius problem asks, given two positive, relatively prime integers $a$ and $b$, what is the maximum integer that cannot be expressed as a linear combination of $a,b$ using non-negative coefficients? This problem is resolved. However, if we add the restriction that the coefficients themselves must also be relatively prime, we create an open problem with connections to the Stern and Farey sequences and the Jacobsthal function. We prove that under this new restriction, a maximum inexpressible integer still always exists, and explore its values and properties as functions of $a,b$.

</details>


### [127] [Higher derivatives of the Gram function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17349)
*Marius Dundulis,Ramūnas Garunkštis,Antanas Laurinčikas,Raivydas Šimėnas*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究Gram函数t_u的高阶导数渐近性质，证明t_u^n的一致分布及连续一致分布（模1），并用AI在Lean中形式化证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Gram函数t_u高阶导数的非线性渐近现象，并建立其幂次t_u^n的一致分布性质，同时借助AI实现证明的形式化验证。

Method: 研究Gram函数t_u高阶导数的渐近非线性现象；对n=1,2,...证明t_u^n的一致分布与连续一致分布（模1）；使用AI在Lean中形式化证明。

Result: 证明了Gram函数t_u高阶导数的非线性渐近现象，以及t_u^n（n=1,2,...）的一致分布和连续一致分布（模1）性质，并完成Lean中的AI形式化。

Conclusion: Gram函数t_u的高阶导数呈现非线性渐近行为，其幂次序列满足（连续）一致分布模1，且相关工作可经AI在Lean中形式化验证。

Abstract: We study the nonlinear phenomenon of the asymptotics of higher-order derivatives of the Gram function $t_u$. We also prove the uniform distribution and continuous uniform distribution modulo one of $t^n_u$ for $n = 1, 2, \dots$. We use AI to formalize our proofs in Lean.

</details>


### [128] [Roth's theorem on weighted projective lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17589)
*Sajad Salami*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究加权射影直线 ${\mathbb P}^1_{\mathfrak q}$ 上有理点逼近的指数问题：全有理轨道集的最优指数为 $2L=2q_0q_1$，而互素整坐标点猜想指数为 $q_0+q_1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过加权 Veronese 映射拉回 Roth 定理可得全有理轨道集的最优指数 $2L$，但互素整坐标表示的点计数提示可能出现更小的指数 $q_0+q_1$，需澄清该差异并建立相应理论。

Method: 构造加权 Veronese 映射 $\Phi$ 满足 $h\circ\Phi=L\,{\mathfrak s}$，拉回 Roth 定理；对互素整坐标点提出指数 $q_0+q_1$ 的猜想，并在 $abc$ 猜想下证明 $S$-adic radical 形式；引入加权 $abc$ 表述，利用加权 Belyi 映射与签名 $(3,2)$ 的系数对等价性进行转移。

Result: 在 $abc$ 猜想下证明了 $S$-adic radical 形式；当 ${\mathfrak q}=(2,3)$ 时目标 $[1:1]$ 处得到互素 Hall 型不等式，Danilov–Elkies 例子表明指数 $5$ 是尖锐的；在特征零函数域上对常数目标与多项式逼近无条件证明了预期指数，并对几乎所有实目标成立。

Conclusion: 应用包括 Schmidt 子空间定理的拉回形式、加权 Thue 不等式，以及 Roth 指数构造的朴素加权重缩放的反例。

Abstract: Let ${\mathbb P}^1_{\mathfrak q}$ be the weighted projective line with coprime weights ${\mathfrak q}=(q_0,q_1)$ and $L=q_0q_1$. The weighted Veronese map $Φ\colon {\mathbb P}^1_{\mathfrak q}\to {\mathbb P}^1$ satisfies $h\circΦ=L\,{\mathfrak s}$. Pulling back Roth's theorem gives the optimal exponent $2L$ for the full rational orbit set. For points represented by coprime integral coordinates, counting suggests the smaller exponent $q_0+q_1$. We formulate this as a conjecture and prove an $S$-adic radical form under the $abc$ conjecture. For ${\mathfrak q}=(2,3)$ the resulting inequality at the target $[1:1]$ is the coprime Hall-type inequality, and Danilov--Elkies examples show that the exponent $5$ is sharp. We also prove the expected exponent unconditionally over characteristic-zero function fields for constant targets and polynomial approximants, and for almost every real target. A weighted $abc$ formulation is introduced; in signature $(3,2)$ finitely many coefficient pairs are equivalent to $abc$, and weighted Belyi maps transfer this input to arbitrary algebraic targets. Applications include a pullback form of Schmidt's subspace theorem, weighted Thue inequalities, and a counterexample to a naive weighted rescaling of Roth's index construction.

</details>


### [129] [The local structure of refined class groups in cyclotomic Iwasawa theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17649)
*Su Hu,Min-Soo Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了分圆Iwasawa理论中ray class精细化(ray class refinement)的显式局部描述。对互异素数 $p\geq5$、$\ell$ 及偶数 $2\leq k\leq p-3$，证明了 $\mathbb{Q}(\zeta_p)$ 的 $p$-primary ray class group 的 $\omega^{1-k}$-分量（平方自由模，支撑在 $\ell$ 上方）非零当且仅当 $p\mid\ell(1-\ell^k)B_k$。在分圆塔中确定了局部模及其关于对角整体Tate模的修正，得到显式行列式公式，并借助Frobenius欧拉因子与Mazur–Wiles定理导出非例外奇特征下ray class形式的Iwasawa主猜想，同时得到各层精确类数赋值比较与 $\mu$-vanishing 精细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Iwasawa主猜想及类数公式主要处理整体不变量，而对ray class group的局部结构、精细化(ray class refinement)以及模平方自由导子的显式判别缺乏统一描述。作者希望给出ray class精细化在分圆Iwasawa理论中的显式局部刻画，从而将判别条件（如 $p\mid\ell(1-\ell^k)B_k$）与整体主猜想、类数赋值及既有Eisenstein同余定理联系起来。

Method: 方法上采用分圆Iwasawa塔中的局部显式描述：计算ray class group相关局部模，并模去对角整体Tate模进行修正，得到显式行列式公式；利用Frobenius欧拉因子结合Mazur–Wiles定理证明Iwasawa主猜想的ray class形式；通过分圆特征解释Wieferich例外，并将可分性判别与素数级Ramanujan型同余及Eisenstein同余定理相连接。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对互异素数 $p\geq5,\ell$ 及偶数 $2\leq k\leq p-3$，$\mathbb{Q}(\zeta_p)$ 的 $p$-primary ray class group 的 $\omega^{1-k}$-分量非零当且仅当 $p\mid\ell(1-\ell^k)B_k$；(2) 在分圆塔中确定局部模及其修正，得到显式行列式公式；(3) 结合Mazur–Wiles定理得到非例外奇特征下ray class形式的Iwasawa主猜想；(4) 获得每层（包括稳定前各层）精确类数赋值比较，恢复 $\ell=2$ 时Finat公式，并从Ferrero–Washington推出精细化 $\mu$-vanishing。

Conclusion: 该工作通过局部分圆Iwasawa描述统一刻画了ray class精细化，得到显式行列式公式与ray class形式的Iwasawa主猜想，并给出精确类数赋值比较、Finat公式的重新导出及精细 $\mu$-vanishing；分圆特征解释了Wieferich例外，且与既有Eisenstein同余定理共同将可分性判别与素数级Ramanujan型同余联系起来。

Abstract: We give an explicit local description of ray class refinements in cyclotomic Iwasawa theory. For distinct primes $p\geq5$ and $\ell$, and even $2\leq k\leq p-3$, we prove that the $ω^{1-k}$-component of the $p$-primary ray class group of $\mathbb{Q}(ζ_p)$, with squarefree modulus supported above $\ell$, is nonzero if and only if $p\mid\ell(1-\ell^k)B_k$. In the cyclotomic tower, we determine the local modules and their correction modulo the diagonal global Tate module, obtaining an explicit determinant formula. Their Frobenius Euler factors, combined with the Mazur--Wiles theorem, yield a ray class form of the Iwasawa main conjecture for nonexceptional odd characters. We also obtain exact class number valuation comparisons at every layer, including those before stabilization, recover Finat's formula for $\ell=2$, and deduce refined $μ$-vanishing from Ferrero--Washington. The cyclotomic character explains the Wieferich exception, while existing Eisenstein congruence theorems connect the divisibility criteria with prime-level Ramanujan-style congruences.

</details>


### [130] [Simultaneously preperiodic points and colliding orbits for families of polynomials in characteristic $p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17650)
*Jungin Lee,GyeongHyeon Nam*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究多项式 f 加上参数 λ 后，两点的前周期点存在无穷多个 λ 的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨参数化多项式族 f(X)+λ 中前周期点的共时性问题，刻画哪些 α,β 能有无穷多个 λ 使其同时前周期。

Method: 在代数闭域 k=F̄_p 及代数闭扩张 L 上，研究 f-f(0) 非加性情形，利用集合 G_f 与 E_f 刻画例外情形。

Result: 证明了：存在无穷多个 λ∈L 使 α,β 同时为 f(X)+λ 的前周期点，当且仅当 α,β∈k 或 f(β)-f(α)∈E_f。

Conclusion: 给出了同时前周期参数集无穷的充要条件，并提供了碰撞轨道集无穷的充要判据。

Abstract: Let $k=\overline{\mathbb{F}}_p$ and let $f\in k[X]$ be a polynomial of degree at least $2$ such that $f-f(0)$ is non-additive. For $α,β$ in an algebraically closed extension $L$ of $k$, we prove that there are infinitely many $λ\in L$ for which both points are preperiodic under $f(X)+λ$ if and only if $α,β\in k$ or $f(β)-f(α)\in E_f$. Here $E_f=\{c\in G_f:(f-f(0))^n(c)\in G_f\text{ for every }n\ge0\}$, where $G_f=\{c\in k : f(X+c)-f(X)\in k\}$. We also provide a necessary and sufficient criterion for the corresponding colliding orbits set to be infinite.

</details>


### [131] [A sharp lower bound for the sextic large sieve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17675)
*Yuanda Xu,Hejian Sang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在Eisenstein整数上建立了六次大筛法在非平衡范围内、指标限制为无平方理想时的匹配下界，得到Ξ₆(A,B)≫(AB)^{-ε}A^{5/6}B^{1/3}，并推出完全截断范数Ξ₆(X,X)≫X^{17/16-ε}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Eisenstein整数上六次剩余特征矩阵的算子范数下界，特别是在行、列指标均限制为无平方理想且范围非平衡的情形，以完善大筛法理论中的匹配界。

Method: 证明一个在素数排除和由Möbius反演产生的特征扭曲下一致的第二矩公式，用以说明无平方投影保留了原矩的正比例；再结合三次与六次大筛界，将下界转移到无平方行上。

Result: 对任意固定0<δ<3/11和ε>0，当B^{16/11+δ}≤A≤B^{2-δ}时有Ξ₆(A,B)≫_{δ,ε}(AB)^{-ε}A^{5/6}B^{1/3}；从而完全截断范数满足Ξ₆(X,X)≫_ε X^{17/16-ε}。

Conclusion: 成功在无平方理想限制下保持了Gauss和提供的大第二矩，获得了六次大筛法的匹配下界，并给出了完全截断范数的下界估计。

Abstract: We establish a matching lower bound for the sextic large sieve over the Eisenstein integers in an unbalanced range, with both indices restricted to squarefree ideals. Let $Ξ_6(A,B)$ denote the squared operator norm of the sextic residue-character matrix with row and column norms at most $A$ and $B$, respectively, outside a fixed finite set of primes containing those above $6$. For every fixed $0<δ<3/11$ and every $\varepsilon>0$, we prove $Ξ_6(A,B)\gg_{δ,\varepsilon}(AB)^{-\varepsilon}A^{5/6}B^{1/3}$ for $B^{16/11+δ}\le A\le B^{2-δ}$. Consequently, the full-cutoff norm satisfies $Ξ_6(X,X)\gg_\varepsilon X^{17/16-\varepsilon}$. The main difficulty is to preserve the large second moment supplied by Gauss sums when the column moduli are restricted to be squarefree. We prove a second-moment formula uniform in the prime exclusions and character twists arising from Möbius inversion, and use it to show that the squarefree projection retains a positive proportion of the original moment. A separate argument combining cubic and sextic large-sieve bounds then transfers the lower bound to squarefree rows.

</details>


### [132] [Yau's number-theoretic conjecture and sharp spectral bounds for quantum harmonic oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17704)
*Zhiwen Liu,Stephen S. -T. Yau,Hao Zuo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Yau关于任意维数实直角单形内正整点数的数论猜想，并将所得尖锐多项式界应用于量子谐振子，得到特征值计数、谱余项及能量和（第一Riesz平均）的显式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau提出关于实直角单形中正整点数的数论猜想，此前缺乏一般维数下的证明；同时量子谐振子的谱计数与能量和需要更尖锐的显式估计，尤其在阈值高于最低特征值的情形。

Method: 基于Yau与Zhang的切片方法（slicing method）进行证明；对有理频率比情形引入额外格坐标与插值处理；对等频率（各向同性）情形利用自然能量网格上的插值。

Result: 在每维n≥3证明了Yau猜想，计数非零时界成立，且等号成立当且仅当所有截距等于同一整数；得到谐振子特征值计数与谱余项的显式上界；有理比频率下（n≥2）给出每个阈值的能量和界；各向同性情形界可达（在能级处精确）；三维精确公式显示Fefferman–Seco共振对应的周期项。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Yau的格点猜想并给出谐振子的尖锐谱界，推导出关于光滑数与格点偏差的算术与几何推论。

Abstract: We prove Yau's number-theoretic conjecture on positive integral points in real right simplices in every dimension $n\ge3$, and apply the resulting sharp polynomial bound to quantum harmonic oscillators. The lattice-point bound holds for every choice of positive real intercepts for which the count is nonzero, with equality precisely when all intercepts equal the same integer. For oscillators in dimensions $n\ge3$, we obtain explicit upper bounds for eigenvalue counts and spectral remainders for arbitrary positive frequencies at every threshold at or above the lowest eigenvalue. For frequencies with rational ratios in dimensions $n\ge2$, an additional lattice coordinate and interpolation give bounds for energy sums, or first Riesz means, at every threshold. When all frequencies are equal (the isotropic case), the oscillator attains these bounds: the counting bound is exact at its energy levels, and interpolation on its natural energy grid recovers the energy sum at every threshold. In three dimensions, the exact formula displays the periodic contribution associated with the resonance discussed by Fefferman and Seco. The proof builds on the slicing method of Yau and Zhang. Arithmetic and geometric consequences include explicit bounds for smooth numbers and lattice-point discrepancies.

</details>


### [133] [A recurrence in logarithmic Möbius sums with coprimality conditions via Euler--Maclaurin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17720)
*Sebastian Zuniga-Alterman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过结合Möbius反演与Euler--Maclaurin求和公式，推导了对数加权Möbius和的递推关系，给出了多项式近似估计，并推广到带互素条件及除数函数倒数加权的和，还证明了后者的非负性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数加权Möbius和的精确估计在数论中有重要作用，但直接计算困难。作者希望建立统一的方法，从低阶估计出发系统性地获得任意对数阶的多项式近似，并将结果推广到更复杂的加权形式（如互素条件、除数函数倒数）。

Method: 将Möbius反演与Euler--Maclaurin求和公式相结合，推导对数加权Möbius和的递推关系；以零阶、一阶、二阶估计为起点，归纳得到任意非负整数对数指数的多项式近似；再通过一个卷积恒等式推广到带互素条件的和，以及对除数函数倒数的加权和。

Result: 获得了对数加权Möbius和在任意非负整数对数阶下的多项式近似估计；将这些估计成功推广到互素条件的情形和除数函数倒数加权的和；并证明后者对每个正整数对数阶都非负，即其符号始终不变。

Conclusion: 该递推方法提供了一个系统性的框架，统一处理各类对数加权Möbius和，并揭示了除数函数倒数加权和的非负性这一新性质。

Abstract: We derive a recurrence for logarithmically weighted Möbius sums by combining Möbius inversion with the Euler--Maclaurin summation formula. Consequently, starting from estimates of orders zero, one and two, we obtain polynomial approximations for these sums for every nonnegative integer exponent of $\log$. We then extend these estimates to sums with coprimality conditions via a convolution identity and to sums weighted by the reciprocal of the sum-of-divisors function. We also establish the nonnegativity of the latter sums for every positive integer logarithmic order, proving that they never change sign.

</details>


### [134] [Fine structure of even Galois deformation rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17865)
*Peter Vang Uttenthal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在特定素导体族中的偶数伽罗瓦表示族，其模3变形环不存在特定形式的商，并由此推出全局偶数变形环在Z_3模范畴中是有限且平坦的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究伽罗瓦变形环的结构是数论与算术几何中的重要问题。作者希望刻画特定层级（level S={ℓ,3}）下偶数模3变形环的性质，从而理解其作为Z_3模的有限性与平坦性。

Method: 设k为满足ℓ≡1 mod 3^k的最大整数k≥1，对每个素导体ℓ的偶数伽罗瓦表示，分析其模3变形环R_S ⊗ F_3的商结构，证明其不存在同构于F_3[T]/(T^{2+3^{k-1}})的商。

Result: 证明了该模3变形环R_S ⊗_{Z_3} F_3不存在F_3[T]/(T^{2+3^{k-1}})这样的商。作为推论，得出全局偶数变形环R_S在Z_3模范畴中是有限且平坦的。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了偶数变形环有限平坦性的一个充分条件，深化了对素导体族中变形环结构的理解。

Abstract: For every even Galois representation in a family of prime conductor $\ell$, we prove that if $k \in \mathbb{Z}_{\geq 1}$ is maximal such that \[\ell \equiv 1 \bmod 3^k\] then the even mod $3$ deformation ring $R_S \otimes_{\mathbb{Z}_3} \mathbb{F}_3$ of level $S=\{\ell,3\}$ does not have a quotient isomorphic to $\mathbb{F}_3[T]/(T^{2+3^{k-1}})$. As a corollary, we deduce that the global even deformation rings $R_S$ are finite and flat in the category of $\mathbb{Z}_3$-modules.

</details>


### [135] [A generating function identity for finite and symmetric multiple zeta values via Dougall's bilateral ${}_2H_2$ summation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17957)
*Takumi Maesaka,Masataka Ono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 利用Dougall双边级数公式建立有限与对称多重zeta值的生成函数恒等式，证明带奇偶限制的受限和公式，并证明Kaneko猜想及其对称版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限多重zeta值与对称多重zeta值的恒等式与猜想，借助Dougall双边2H2求和公式寻找统一方法。

Method: 使用Dougall双边2H2求和公式，建立有限与对称多重zeta值的生成函数恒等式，并应用于受限和公式及Kaneko猜想。

Result: 得到生成函数恒等式；证明各分量奇偶性被指定的受限和公式；导出有限与对称多重zeta值ζ_A({1,2}^n)、ζ_S({1,2}^n)的生成函数恒等式，证明Kaneko猜想及其对称类比。

Conclusion: Dougall双边求和公式为多重zeta值提供有效工具，统一处理受限和公式与Kaneko猜想。

Abstract: We establish a generating function identity for finite and symmetric multiple zeta values using Dougall's bilateral ${}_2H_2$ summation formula. As an application, we prove a restricted sum formula in which the parity of each component of the index is prescribed. We also derive a generating function identity for finite multiple zeta values $ζ^{}_{\mathcal{A}}(\{1,2\}^n)$ and symmetric multiple zeta values $ζ^{}_{\mathcal{S}}(\{1,2\}^n)$, thereby proving Kaneko's conjecture for finite multiple zeta values and its symmetric analogue.

</details>


### [136] [On Berndt-Straub conjectures about zeros of generalized Ramanujan polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18065)
*Siddhi Pathak*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Berndt-Straub关于广义Ramanujan多项式零点分布的两个猜想中的第一个（对$R_k$成立），并否定了第二个：$S_{2n}$偏离单位圆的非零零点至多有四个且均为纯虚数，该界在$\chi_{163}$、$S_6$情形下可取到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berndt与Straub曾猜想广义Ramanujan多项式$R_k(x;\chi,\psi)$与$S_{2n}(x;\chi,\chi)$（实非主Dirichlet特征）的所有非零零点都位于单位圆上，这被称为"周期多项式的Riemann假设"。本文旨在判定这两个猜想的真伪，并刻画例外零点的数量、位置与渐近行为。

Method: 对$R_k$直接证明其零点全部落在单位圆上；对$S_{2n}$证明至多四个非零零点偏离单位圆且为纯虚数。通过取模163的本原二次特征$\chi_{163}=\left(\frac{\cdot}{163}\right)$构造$S_6(x;\chi_{163},\chi_{163})$的四个例外零点以说明界是sharp的。进而引入一个超越不变量，用其符号判定例外零点是否在所有充分大的次数中出现，并研究这些零点向单位圆外超越极限的收敛；对奇特征情形，将该不变量和极限与Fekete多项式相联系。

Result: $R_k$的猜想成立，其非零零点均在单位圆上。$S_{2n}$的猜想不成立：至多四个非零零点落在单位圆外且为纯虚数，且$\chi_{163}$时$S_6$恰有四个例外零点，说明该上界是最优的。新引入的超越不变量的符号决定了充分大次数下例外零点是否出现，且这些零点收敛到单位圆外的超越极限。

Conclusion: "周期多项式的Riemann假设"对Eisenstein新形式可能失效；例外零点现象由一新超越不变量控制，其存在性与渐近位置可被精确刻画，并与Fekete多项式相关联。

Abstract: We settle two conjectures of Berndt and Straub asserting that all nonzero zeros of the generalized Ramanujan polynomials $R_k(x;χ,ψ)$ and $S_{2n}(x;χ,χ)$ lie on the unit circle for real nonprincipal Dirichlet characters. We prove their conjecture for $R_k$. For $S_{2n}$, we prove that at most four nonzero zeros lie off the unit circle, all purely imaginary. If $χ_{163}=\left(\frac{\cdot}{163}\right)$ is the primitive quadratic character modulo $163$, then $S_6(x;χ_{163},χ_{163})$ has four exceptional zeros, showing that this bound is sharp. This disproves the second conjecture and establishes that the `Riemann hypothesis for period polynomials' can fail for Eisenstein newforms. Further, we introduce a transcendental invariant whose sign determines whether exceptional zeros of $S_{2n}(x;χ,χ)$ occur in all sufficiently large degrees, and show that these zeros converge to transcendental limits off the unit circle. For primitive odd characters, we relate this invariant and these limits to Fekete polynomials.

</details>


### [137] [A certified classical-strip comparison for the de Bruijn-Newman constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18214)
*Michel Planat*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文在经典零点条带框架下，对 de Bruijn–Newman 热变形进行有向比较，在明确的模型、局部化与全局接触前提下，用平移探针估计得到 Λ ≤ 0.148611789321711 < 0.148612，并通过高精度尾端包围与精确有理审计验证全部比较步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 de Bruijn–Newman 常数的上界，把经典零点条带下的比较推进到更紧的数值界，并厘清更强指数对所需的有限条件。

Method: 从经典零点条带 |Im z| ≤ 1 出发，在完整模型到函数来源、局部化与全局接触前提下使用平移探针估计；结合 Raymond 有限源数据与 57 个固定平移核，用有理 Bernstein 证书独立检查；以有限 Weyl 估计改进正首尾与中心对数矩，并通过中心锥转移保留误差额度与锥储备。

Result: 得到 Λ ≤ 0.148611789321711 < 0.148612；尾端包围在 192 与 256 位重新生成，精确有理审计器接受全部 19,023 步比较；相对完整双源比较的受控下降为 0.000021760452379；另证明匹配的对数尾转移并给出更强指数对的有限要求。

Conclusion: 在所述前提与审计下，de Bruijn–Newman 常数上界被改进至低于 0.148612；附录还记录在另一 7/8 无零假设下 Raymond 的下界 0.116398。

Abstract: We give a directed comparison beginning with the classical zero strip \(\lvert\Ima z\rvert\le1\) for the de Bruijn--Newman heat deformation. Under explicitly stated complete model-to-function source, localisation and global contact premises, translated-probe estimates imply \[
  Λ\le\frac{148611789321711}{10^{15}}
  =0.148611789321711<0.148612. \] The construction uses Raymond's finite source data and 57 fixed translated kernels, independently checked by rational Bernstein certificates. An explicit finite Weyl estimate improves the positive first-head tail and its centred logarithmic moment. A centred cone transfer retains the original physical error allowances, exceptional debit and additive cone reserve, and charges the change in existing phase-surplus credits. Directed tail enclosures are regenerated at 192 and 256 bits; a separate exact-rational auditor accepts all 19,023 comparison steps. The controlled decrease from the complete two-source comparison is \(0.000021760452379\). We also prove a compatible matched logarithmic tail transfer and identify the finite requirements for stronger exponent pairs. A supplementary section records Raymond's ceiling below \(0.116398\) under the distinct \(7/8\) zero-free hypothesis.

</details>


### [138] [Higher rank elliptic sieve via CM endomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18281)
*Hang Lok Edison Au-Yeung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了带复乘的椭圆曲线上几乎所有的倍数点的 y 坐标不属于某个阿贝尔扩张的范数群，推广了 Landau–Ramanujan 关于几乎所有整数都不是两平方和（即高斯有理数范数）的经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典定理（Landau–Ramanujan）指出几乎所有整数都不是两平方和，等价地，几乎所有整数都不是高斯有理数 Q(i) 的范数。作者希望将这一"范数结果"推广到具复乘的椭圆曲线的情形，考察几乎所有的 P 的倍数点的 y 坐标是否落在某个阿贝尔扩张 L/K 的范数群中。既有工作 Bhakta 等人仅处理低秩情形，无法应对由额外自同态产生的点，因此需要新的技术方案。

Method: 设 E 为数域 K 上具复乘的椭圆曲线，且 K 包含 E 的自同态环。固定非挠点 P ∈ E(K) 及阿贝尔扩张 L/K。在满足一个技术性条件的前提下，作者改进了 Bhakta 等人的论证方法，主要工具是椭圆可除序列（elliptic divisibility sequences）和称为椭圆筛法（elliptic sieve）的筛术。为处理由额外自同态在 E(K) 中产生的点，作者把椭圆筛法应用于椭圆网（elliptic nets）——椭圆可除序列的高秩推广。

Result: 在满足技术条件的情况下，几乎所有的 P 的倍数点的 y 坐标都不属于 L 的范数群。这一结果把 Landau–Ramanujan 型范数结论从 Q(i) 推广到具复乘的椭圆曲线上，且覆盖了因额外自同态而产生的更高秩情形。

Conclusion: 该工作表明椭圆筛法可以应用于椭圆曲线上点的 Z-线性组合，同时展示了椭圆网在椭圆曲线计数问题中的实用性，为将筛法技术推广到更高秩的算术问题提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A theorem of Landau and Ramanujan states that almost all integers are not sums of two squares (when ordered by their absolute value). Equivalently, almost all integers are not norms from the Gaussian rationals $\mathbb{Q}(i)$. In this paper, we prove a version of this norm result for elliptic curves $E$. Let $E$ be an elliptic curve over a number field $K$ with complex multiplication, such that $K$ contains the endomorphism ring of $E$. For a fixed non-torsion point $P \in E(K)$ and an abelian extension $L/K$, we show that subject to a technical condition, the $y$-coordinate of almost all multiples of $P$ does not belong to the norm group of $L$. The proof is an adaptation of an argument of Bhakta et al. , whose main tools are elliptic divisibility sequences and a sieve technique known as the elliptic sieve. To account for the points in $E(K)$ arising from the additional endomorphisms, we apply the elliptic sieve to elliptic nets, a higher-rank generalisation of elliptic divisibility sequences. This approach demonstrates the possibility of applying the elliptic sieve to $\mathbb{Z}$--linear combination of points on elliptic curves and the usefulness of elliptic nets to counting problems on elliptic curves.

</details>


### [139] [Untrodden pathways in the study of partitions with parts separated by parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18319)
*Nargish Punia,Rahul Kumar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究与Andrews 2019年引入的一类分拆函数相关的按奇偶分离部分的分拆函数，用模拟模形式刻画其生成函数，并研究其最小部分类比，给出组合解释与渐近估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andrews在2019年引入了一类含“按奇偶分离部分”的分拆函数，该方向已有一定研究积累但尚不充分。作者希望在该框架下进一步研究其中一类分拆函数，并首次考察其“最小部分”类比，以揭示其与模拟模形式、模拟Maass theta函数及Zagier q-级数的联系，并获得新的组合与渐近结果。

Method: 将目标分拆函数的生成函数表示为权3/2的模拟模形式；引入并研究按奇偶分离部分分拆函数的最小部分类比，把对应生成函数用模拟模形式、模拟Maass theta函数及Zagier q-级数H(q)表示；通过q-级数恒等式与Ferrers图（奇Ferrers图）方法建立系数的组合解释，并推导其中一个分拆函数生成函数的渐近主项。

Result: 证明了该分拆函数的生成函数可表示为权3/2的模拟模形式；其最小部分类比的生成函数可由模拟模形式、模拟Maass theta函数与Zagier q-级数H(q)（被称为Dedekind eta函数的“半导数”）表示；用奇Ferrers图给出了上述模拟模形式、函数σ*(q)以及模拟theta函数ψ(q)、A(q)系数的新组合解释；给出了一个涉及Ramanujan σ(q)函数的量子q-级数恒等式；推导出其中一个分拆函数生成函数（先表示为σ*(q)）的渐近主项。

Conclusion: 该研究把按奇偶分离部分的分拆函数纳入模拟模形式框架，并开创了其最小部分类比的研究，拓展了模拟模形式、模拟Maass theta函数与q-级数之间的关联，同时提供了新的Ferrers图组合解释、量子q-级数恒等式及渐近结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a partition function with parts separated by parity from a family of partition functions introduced by Andrews in 2019. We first show that its generating function admit representation in terms of mock modular forms of weight $3/2$. We then initiate a study of smallest-part partition function analogues of partitions with parts separated by parity, which, to the best of our knowledge, has not previously been investigated in the literature. We show that the generating functions of these smallest-part partition functions are likewise expressible in terms of mock modular forms, mock Maass theta functions and Zagier's $q$-series $H(q)$, which he describes as a ``half-derivative of the Dedekind eta-function". Our study also leads to new combinatorial interpretations of the coefficients of the aforementioned mock modular form, function $σ^*(q)$, and mock theta functions $ψ(q)$ and $A(q)$ in terms of odd Ferrers graphs. Moreover, we provide a quantum $q$-series identity involving Ramanujan's famous $σ(q)$ function. We also derive the leading asymptotic term of one of these partition functions, whose generating function we first express in terms of $σ^*(q)$.

</details>


### [140] [A Combinatorial Proof of Partition Identities for the Number of Distinct Part Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18371)
*Archit Agarwal,Meghali Garg*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了Bressoud–Subbarao型加权分拆恒等式的一个组合证明，并将其推广到$p^{(k)}(n)$（恰好含$k$种不同部分大小的分拆数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1984年Bressoud与Subbarao建立了联系分拆函数与除子函数的加权分拆恒等式，近期 Bhoria、Eyyunni、Maji 与第一作者将之推广得到关于$p^{(2)}(n)$的类似恒等式，但该恒等式缺乏组合证明，本文旨在填补这一空白并进一步推广。

Method: 采用组合方法（双射/符号反转等组合论证）对已有的$p^{(2)}(n)$恒等式给出组合证明，并将结论推广至一般情形$p^{(k)}(n)$。

Result: 成功给出了$p^{(2)}(n)$加权分拆恒等式的组合证明，并将其推广到$p^{(k)}(n)$，即分拆成恰好$k$个不同部分大小的分拆数的情形。

Conclusion: 该恒等式具有清晰的组合解释，且其形式可自然推广到含恰好$k$种不同部分大小的分拆数。

Abstract: In 1984, Bressoud and Subbarao established an interesting weighted partition identity connecting the partition function and the divisor function. Recently, the first author, jointly with Bhoria, Eyyunni, and Maji, extended the work of Bressoud and Subbarao and obtained the following analogous weighted partition identity. For any $n \in \mathbb{N}$, they obtained \begin{equation*} p^{(2)}(n) = \sum_{π\in \mathcal{D}(n)} (-1)^{ \# (π) }s(π) \left( \ell(π) - s(π) \right). \end{equation*} In the present paper, we provide a combinatorial proof of the above identity and extend the result for $p^{(k)}(n)$, where $p^{(k)}(n)$ denotes the number of partitions of $n$ into exactly $k$ distinct part sizes.

</details>


### [141] [On Permutations Whose Adjacent-Sum Reciprocals Sum to One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18431)
*Jinggao Ai,Dingsheng Hu,Junmin Yan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造性地证明了孙智伟提出的猜想：对任意整数 n>7（圆周排列）或 n>6（线性排列），可将 1,2,...,n 排列使得所有相邻数之和的倒数之和为 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明孙智伟提出的关于倒数相邻和排列的猜想。

Method: 按 n mod 12 分成九个剩余类，证明一个递归命题，并归纳构造新例子；引言中的主要引理和命题在第二节证明，其余情形由附录中的显式构造处理。

Result: 对所有 n>7（圆周）和 n>6（线性）构造出满足条件的排列。

Conclusion: 该猜想得到完整证明，方法为递归与显式构造相结合。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a constructive proof that for any integer n>7 (resp. n>6), the numbers 1,2,...,n can be arranged in a circle (resp. in a line) such that the sum of the reciprocals of all adjacent sums equals 1. The above conjecture was originally posed by Zhi-Wei Sun. Partitioning the integers according to n mod 12 into nine residue classes, we prove a recursive proposition and inductively construct new examples. Main lemmas and propositions in introduction are proved in Section 2. The rest of the cases are handled by explicit constructions in appendix.

</details>


### [142] [Some applications of the quasi-Riemann hypothesis to class groups and small generators of number fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18491)
*Christopher Frei,Efthymios Sofos,Martin Widmer*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在拟黎曼假设下证明了Chebotarev密度定理的有效版本，并将其应用于类群$\ell$-挠、最小分裂素数及数域中的小生成元等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将Chebotarev密度定理的误差项做到显式有效，从而在条件性假设下推广和改进数域类群挠结构及相关素数分布的结果。

Method: 在Dedekind zeta函数的quasi-Riemann假设下证明有效Chebotarev密度定理，并借鉴OpenAI与Cureton--Thorner的近期突破，对Ellenberg--Venkatesh关于类群$\ell$-挠的界做无条件化处理，并将Lemke Oliver--Smith的平均结果推广到任意$d$次数域族。

Result: 得到了有效Chebotarev密度定理；使Ellenberg--Venkatesh的类群$\ell$-挠界变为无条件；将Lemke Oliver--Smith的平均结果推广至任意$d$次数域族；并给出关于最小分裂素数与数域中小生成元的应用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明有效Chebotarev密度定理是研究类群挠结构与数域算术性质的有力工具，并可在条件性假设与近期技术进展下得到一系列改进的定量结果。

Abstract: We prove an effective version of the Chebotarev density theorem, conditional on the quasi-Riemann hypothesis for the Dedekind zeta function. Drawing on recent breakthroughs of OpenAI and Cureton--Thorner, we make unconditional the Ellenberg--Venkatesh bounds on $\ell$-torsion in class groups of number fields and extend an averaged result of Lemke Oliver--Smith to arbitrary families of degree-$d$-fields. Further applications are given to the least prime splitting and to small generators in number fields.

</details>


### [143] [Linking numbers and special differential characters in arithmetic hyperbolic manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18503)
*Mads Bjerge Christensen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了一类非全纯Siegel模形式，其傅里叶系数编码算术双曲流形中的环绕数，并给出Kudla–Millson理论中转移形式（transgression forms）的深入理解及其所满足的流方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环绕数是拓扑与几何中的重要不变量，而将其与自守形式的系数相联系，能在数论、表示论与几何拓扑之间架起桥梁。作者希望推广Kudla–Millson理论，使其能够处理算术双曲流形中最简单类型流形上的环绕数，从而获得新的自守形式构造。

Method: 通过改进对Kudla–Millson理论中出现的转移形式（transgression forms）的理解，利用这些形式的几何与解析性质（包括其流方程）来构造非全纯Siegel模形式，并将其系数与算术双曲流形中的环绕数关联起来。

Result: 成功构造出系数编码环绕数的非全纯Siegel模形式，并建立了转移形式的结构性质，特别是证明了其满足预期的流方程（current equations）。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了Kudla–Millson理论的应用范围，揭示了Siegel模形式与算术双曲流形拓扑不变量（环绕数）之间的深刻联系，为后续研究提供了新的工具与视角。

Abstract: We construct non-holomorphic Siegel modular forms whose coefficients encode linking numbers in arithmetic hyperbolic manifolds of simplest type. Our proofs rely on an improved understanding of the transgression forms appearing in Kudla--Millson theory, and along the way we establish some structural properties including in particular their expected current equations.

</details>


### [144] [Wendt's determinant and finiteness of prime characteristics for permutation binomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18584)
*Zhichao Tang,Xiang Fan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有限域上形如 $X^r(X^s+a)$ 的置换二项式，证明固定 $g=\gcd(s,p^m-1)$ 时只有有限个素特征 $p$ 允许此类置换二项式，并给出可容许特征的显式判定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置换多项式在有限域理论中有重要地位，而形如 $X^r(X^s+a)$ 的置换二项式是其中一类自然的研究对象。作者希望刻画哪些素特征 $p$ 能允许此类置换二项式存在，从而将问题归结为有限多个特征，并给出可计算的判定条件。

Method: 令 $g=\gcd(s,p^m-1)$。对固定的 $g$，利用 Wendt 行列式 $W_g=\operatorname{Res}_X(X^g-1,(X+1)^g-1)$ 进行整除性分析：当 $6\nmid g$ 时证明每个允许的 $p$ 整除非零的 $W_g$；当 $6\mid g$ 时 $W_g$ 为零，于是改用逆幂和与迭代结式构造一个显式非零整数，使其被所有满足 $p>2g+1$ 的允许特征整除。此外还借助进一步的整除条件处理素域情形。

Result: 证明了对每个固定 $g$，仅有有限多个素特征 $p$ 允许此类置换二项式（即使 $m,r,s,a$ 任意变化）。给出了 $1\leqslant g\leqslant10$ 时精确的可容许特征列表；得到了素域上的进一步整除条件；并证明在素域中 $g=11$ 恰好对应 $p=23$。

Conclusion: 该工作将置换二项式存在性问题转化为对固定 $g$ 的有限素特征判定问题，并通过 Wendt 行列式、逆幂和与迭代结式给出了显式且可计算的判定工具，同时完整确定了 $g\le 10$ 的情形及素域中 $g=11$ 的特殊情况。

Abstract: For $f(X)=X^r(X^s+a)\in\mathbb F_{p^m}[X]$, with $m,r,s\geqslant1$ and $a\in\mathbb F_{p^m}^\times$, put $g=\gcd(s,p^m-1)$. We prove that, for each fixed $g$, only finitely many prime characteristics $p$ admit such permutation binomials, even as $m,r,s,a$ vary. If $6\nmid g$, every such $p$ divides Wendt's determinant $W_g=\operatorname{Res}_X(X^g-1,(X+1)^g-1)$, which is nonzero. When $6\mid g$, this determinant vanishes; inverse power sums and iterated resultants yield an explicit nonzero integer divisible by every such $p>2g+1$. We determine the exact admissible characteristics for $1\leqslant g\leqslant10$, obtain a further divisibility condition over prime fields, and show that in prime fields $g=11$ occurs precisely when $p=23$.

</details>


### [145] [Geometric bounds on norms of signed indecomposable integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18737)
*Siu Hang Man,Zdeněk Pezlar,Pavlo Yatsyna*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过几何论证，给出了全实代数数域中带符号不可分解整数的范数界，并改进了Tinková和Voutier关于实二次域中全正不可分解整数范数的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可分解整数（indecomposable integers）在代数数论中具有重要意义，其范数界的刻画有助于理解数域中整数环的分解结构。此前已有关于全正不可分解整数范数界的结果，但带符号情形以及界的精确性仍有改进空间。

Method: 采用几何论证（geometric argument）的方法，建立全实代数数域中带符号不可分解整数的范数界；同时在实二次域情形下，对已有方法进行改进以得到更优的全正不可分解整数范数上界。

Result: 得到了全实代数数域中带符号不可分解整数范数的界，并且改进了Tinková和Voutier给出的实二次域中全正不可分解整数的范数上界。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了不可分解整数范数界的研究范围（带符号情形），并在实二次域情形下给出了更精确的界，为后续相关研究提供了新的工具与结果。

Abstract: We establish bounds on the norm of signed indecomposable integers in totally real number fields, via a geometric argument. We also improve the bound on the norm of totally positive indecomposable integers in real quadratic fields by Tinková and Voutier.

</details>


### [146] [Counting quaternion extensions by multi-heights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18815)
*Willem Hansen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Gundlach关于有理数域$\QQ$上以$Q_8$为Galois群、具有有界多重不变量的扩张数目的猜想的一个情形，并在野分歧处施加局部条件下得到结果，进而给出以分歧素数乘积为界的$Q_8$-扩张数目的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gundlach曾猜想有理数域$\QQ$上固定Galois群、多重不变量有界的扩张数目。该猜想一般情形尚未解决，作者希望针对四元数群$Q_8$这一非交换且具有代表性的情形进行验证，并进一步研究野分歧处的局部条件对计数的影响。

Method: 作者采用数域上Galois扩张的计数方法，结合分歧理论处理野分歧处的局部条件，对$G=Q_8$的情形进行证明，并将结果应用于以分歧素数乘积为界的扩张计数。

Result: 证明了Gundlach猜想在$G=Q_8$时的一个情形；在野分歧处施加局部条件时也获得了相应结果；作为应用，得到了以分歧素数乘积为界的$Q_8$-扩张数目的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了Gundlach猜想在$Q_8$情形下的正确性，拓展了带局部条件的分歧计数结果，并给出有界分歧素数乘积下$Q_8$-扩张的渐近分布。

Abstract: We prove a case of a conjecture of Gundlach on the number of Galois extensions over $\QQ$ with bounded multi-invariants for the group $G=Q_8$. We also obtain results when imposing local conditions at the wildly ramified place. As an application, we prove asymptotics for the number of $Q_8$-extensions with bounded product of ramified primes.

</details>
